\n= 1 - 2\" id=\"MathJax-Element-60-Frame\" role=\"presentation\" style=\"position: relative;\" tabindex=\"0\">2–√2\n\n, எனில் x3\" id=\"MathJax-Element-61-Frame\" role=\"presentation\" style=\"position: relative;\" tabindex=\"0\">x3x3\n\n+ 1x3\" id=\"MathJax-Element-62-Frame\" role=\"presentation\" style=\"position: relative;\" tabindex=\"0\">1x31x3\n\n-ன் மதிப்பைக் கண்டறியவும்.\n2f:T65b,\n4 - 2 Shortcut Trick\n

If x + 1/x = k, then x3 + 1/x3 = k(k2 − 3).

\n

Given k = 1 − √2, we first find k2 = (1 − √2)2 = 3 − 2√2.

\n

Then, k2 − 3 = 3 − 2√2 − 3 = −2√2.

\n

Now multiply by k: (1 − √2) × (−2√2) = −2√2 + 4 = 4 − 2√2.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 4 − 2√2.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

x + 1/x = 1 − √2

\n

Formula Used:

\n

(a + b)3 = a3 + b3 + 3ab(a + b)

\n

⇒ x3 + 1/x3 = (x + 1/x)3 − 3(x + 1/x)

\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ Let x + 1/x = k = 1 − √2

\n

⇒ x3 + 1/x3 = k3 − 3k = k(k2 − 3)

\n

⇒ Now, squaring both sides of k = 1 − √2

\n

⇒ k2 = 12 + (√2)2 − 2 × 1 × √2

\n

⇒ k2 = 1 + 2 − 2√2 = 3 − 2√2

\n

⇒ Now compute the term (k2 − 3):

\n

⇒ k2 − 3 = (3 − 2√2) − 3 = −2√2

\n

⇒ Substitute the values back into the expression k(k2 − 3):

\n

⇒ x3 + 1/x3 = (1 − √2) × (−2√2)

\n

⇒ x3 + 1/x3 = (1 × −2√2) − (√2 × −2√2)

\n

⇒ x3 + 1/x3 = −2√2 − (−4) = 4 − 2√2

\n

∴ The correct answer is 4 − 2√2.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

Cube of a Binomial

\n

The formula for the sum of cubes is derived from: (a + b)3 = a3 + b3 + 3ab(a + b). Rearranging this gives a3 + b3 = (a + b)3 − 3ab(a + b).

\n

Algebraic Identities for Squares

\n

If x + 1/x = k, then x2 + 1/x2 = k2 − 2. This identity is extremely useful for stepping up to higher even powers directly.

\n

Algebraic Identities for Differences

\n

If x − 1/x = k, then the corresponding difference formulas are x3 − 1/x3 = k3 + 3k, and x2 + 1/x2 = k2 + 2.

∴ अभीष्ट उत्तर 4 - 2 \\(\\sqrt2\\) है।

ఒక్కొక్కటి 200 గ్రాముల బరువున్న 10 సెంటీమీటర్ల సైడ్ క్లోజ్డ్ క్యూబ్ ను తయారు చేస్తారు. ఈ క్యూబ్ ను సమాంతర ముఖంతో నీటిలో ఉంచుతారు. నీటి సాంద్రత సెం.మీ.కు 1 గ్రాము అయితే దాని ఎత్తు ఎన్ని  సెం.మీ నీటిలో మునిగిపోతుంది?

\n", "options": [{"prompt": "1", "value": "1 సెం.మీ"}, {"prompt": "2", "value": "1.5 సెం.మీ"}, {"prompt": "3", "value": "2 సెం.మీ"}, {"prompt": "4", "value": "2.5 సెం.మీ"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}}, "correct_option": "3", "solution_html": {"en": "\n

Shortcut Trick\n

According to the Law of Flotation, Weight of a floating object = Weight of displaced liquid.

\n

Base Area × Submerged Height × Density of liquid = Weight of object

\n

⇒ (10 × 10) × h × 1 = 200

\n

⇒ 100 × h = 200 ⇒ h = 2 cm

\n

∴ The correct answer is 2 cm.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

Side of the closed cube (a) = 10 cm

\n

Weight of the cube (W) = 200 gm

\n

Density of water (ρ) = 1 gm/cm3

\n\n\n\n

Formula Used:

\n

Volume of a cuboid = Base Area × Height

\n

Weight = Volume × Density

\n

Law of Flotation: A floating body displaces its own weight of the fluid in which it floats.

\n\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ Base area of the cube = Side × Side = 10 × 10 = 100 cm2

\n

⇒ Let the height of the cube that sinks in water be h cm.

\n

⇒ Volume of water displaced = Base area × Submerged height = 100 × h = 100h cm3

\n

⇒ Weight of displaced water = Volume of displaced water × Density of water

\n

⇒ Weight of displaced water = 100h × 1 = 100h gm

\n

According to Archimedes' Principle:

\n

⇒ Weight of the cube = Weight of the displaced water

\n

⇒ 200 = 100h

\n

⇒ h = 200 ÷ 100 = 2 cm

\n

∴ The correct answer is 2 cm.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

Archimedes' Principle

\n

Any object wholly or partially immersed in a fluid experiences an upward buoyant force equal to the weight of the fluid displaced by the object.

\n

Law of Flotation

\n

An object will float if its average density is less than the density of the fluid. In equilibrium, the floating object displaces a weight of fluid strictly equal to its own weight.

\n

Density and Volume Relation

\n

Density (ρ) is defined as mass (or weight in a uniform gravitational field) per unit volume. Calculated as Weight = Volume × Density.

दिया गया है:

\n\n

घन की भुजा (l) = 10 सेमी

\n\n

घन का वज़न (W) = 200 ग्राम

\n\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

\n\n

भार घनत्व = \\(\\dfrac{Weight}{Volume}\\)

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

मान लीजिए कि घन d सेमी तक डूब जाता है।

\n\n

घन का वज़न = विस्थापित जल का वज़न 

\n\n

फलक का पृष्ठीय क्षेत्रफल = 10 × 10 = 100 सेमी2

\n\n

विस्थापित जल का आयतन = 100 × d सेमी3

\n\n

जल का घनत्व = 1 ग्राम/सेमी3

\n\n

विस्थापित जल का वज़न = 100 × d × 1  = 100d g

\n\n

दोनों वज़नों को बराबर करने पर,

\n\n

⇒ 100d = 200

\n\n

⇒ d = 2 सेमी 

\n\n

∴ उत्तर 2 सेमी है।

Shortcut Trick\n

Each of the 3 friends has 2 choices of whom to throw the ball to, making the total possible outcomes = 23 = 8.

ఇచ్చినది:

\n\n

ఘనం (l) = 10 సెం.మీ

\n\n

ఘనం (W) బరువు = 200 గ్రా

\n\n

ఉపయోగించిన సూత్రం:

\n\n

బరువు సాంద్రత = \\(\\dfrac{Weight}{Volume}\\)

\n\n

గణన:

\n\n

d cm వరకు క్యూబ్ సింక్ అయ్యేలా చూసుకో౦డి.

\n\n

ఘనం యొక్క బరువు = స్థానభ్రంశం చేయబడిన నీటి బరువు

\n\n

ముఖం యొక్క ఉపరితల వైశాల్యం = 10 × 10 = 100 సెం.మీ2

\n\n

స్థానభ్రంశం చెందిన నీటి ఘనపరిమాణం = 100 × d సెం.మీ3

\n\n

నీటి సాంద్రత = 1 గ్రాములు / సెం.మీ3

\n\n

స్థానభ్రంశం చేయబడిన నీటి బరువు = 100 × d × 1 = 100d g

\n\n

రెండు బరువులను సమానం చేయండి

\n\n

⇒ 100d = 200

\n\n

⇒ d = 2 సెం.మీ.

\n\n

∴ ఆన్సర్ ఐడీ 2 సెం.మీ.

Shortcut Trick\n

Using the direct formula for Time in Simple Interest:

\n

Time = (SI × 100) ÷ (Principal × Rate)

\n

T = (54 × 100) ÷ (600 × 3)

\n

T = 5400 ÷ 1800 = 3

\n

∴ The correct answer is 3 years.

\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

Principal (P) = Rs. 600

\n

Simple Interest (SI) = Rs. 54

\n

Rate of Interest (R) = 3% per annum

\n

Formula Used:

\n

SI = (P × R × T) ÷ 100

\n\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ 54 = (600 × 3 × T) ÷ 100

\n

⇒ 54 = 6 × 3 × T

\n

⇒ 54 = 18 × T

\n

⇒ T = 54 ÷ 18

\n

⇒ T = 3

\n

∴ The correct answer is 3 years.

\n

Additional Information

\n

Simple Interest (SI)

\n

Simple Interest is calculated uniformly only on the initial principal amount throughout the entire time period. The fundamental formula is SI = (P × R × T) ÷ 100.

\n

Total Amount (A)

\n

The total amount to be paid or received at the end of the specified time period is the sum of the Principal and Simple Interest. Formula: A = P + SI.

\n

Simple vs Compound Interest

\n

While Simple Interest remains constant every year for a given principal and rate, Compound Interest is continuously calculated on both the initial principal and the accumulated interest of the previous periods.

\n\n

ब्याज = रु. 54

दर = 3% प्रति वर्ष

सूत्र:

\n\n

साधारण ब्याज = मूलधन × दर × समय/100

\n\n

समय = साधारण ब्याज / (मूलधन × दर/100)

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

समय = रु. 54/(रु. 600 × 3/100) = 3 वर्ष

\n\n

इसलिए, राशि प्राप्त करने में 3 साल लगेंगे। ब्याज के रूप में 54 रु.

Shortcut Trick\n

Let the total area of ΔPQR be 30 units (using the LCM of denominator values 6 and 5).

\n

Area of ΔPXZ = (1/2) × (1/3) × 30 = 5 units.

\n

Area of ΔXQY = (1/2) × (2/5) × 30 = 6 units.

\n

Area of quadrilateral XYRZ = Area of ΔPQR − (Area of ΔPXZ + Area of ΔXQY) = 30 − (5 + 6) = 19 units.

\n

Required Ratio = Area of XYRZ ÷ Area of ΔPXZ = 19 / 5.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 19/5.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

PX : XQ = 1 : 1 ⇒ PX / PQ = 1 / 2 and XQ / PQ = 1 / 2

\n

PZ : ZR = 1 : 2 ⇒ PZ / PR = 1 / 3

\n

QY : YR = 2 : 3 ⇒ QY / QR = 2 / 5

\n\nदिया गया:

\n\n

PX ∶ XQ = 1 ∶ 1, PZ ∶ ZR = 1 ∶ 2, QY ∶ YR = 2 ∶ 3.

\n\n

प्रयुक्त अवधारणा:

\n\n

Δ ABC में, Δ ABC का क्षेत्रफल = (1/2)AB × AC × SynA

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

\n\n

चरण 1: PXZ : PQR के क्षेत्रफल का अनुपात ज्ञात कीजिए

\n\n

\\(\\rm\\frac{\\text { Area of } \\triangle P X Z}{\\text { Area of } \\triangle P Q R}=\\frac{\\frac{1}{2} × P X . P Z \\cdot \\sin P}{\\frac{1}{2} × P Q . P R \\cdot \\sin P}\\) = \\(\\rm\\left(\\frac{P X}{P Q} × \\frac{P Z}{P R}\\right)=\\left(\\frac{1}{2} × \\frac{1}{3}\\right)\\)

\n\n

⇒ ar (Δ PXZ) / ar ( Δ PQR) = 1/6 ---(1)

\n\n

चरण 2: QXY : PQR के क्षेत्रफल का अनुपात ज्ञात कीजिए

\n\n

\\(\\rm\\frac{\\text { Area of } \\triangle Q X Y}{\\text { Area of } \\triangle P Q R}=\\frac{\\frac{1}{2} Q X \\cdot Q Y \\cdot \\sin Q}{\\frac{1}{2} \\cdot P Q \\cdot Q R \\cdot \\sin Q}\\) = \\(\\rm\\frac{Q X}{P Q} × \\frac{Q Y}{Q R}=\\frac{1}{2} × \\frac{2}{5}\\)

\n\n

⇒ ar (Δ QXY) / ar ( Δ PQR) = 1/5 ---(2)

\n\n

अब, दोनों समीकरणों में Δ PQR का क्षेत्रफल समान करें,

\n\n

ar (Δ PXZ) / ar (Δ PQR) = 1/6 × 5/5 = 5/30

\n\n

ar (Δ QXY) / ar (Δ PQR) = 1/5 × 6/6 = 6/30

\n\n

यहाँ, दोनों स्थितियों में Δ PQR का क्षेत्रफल 30 वर्ग इकाई है।

\n\n

अतः Δ PXZ का क्षेत्रफल = 5 इकाई2

\n\n

तथा, Δ QXY का क्षेत्रफल = 6 इकाई2

\n\n

अतः, XYRZ का क्षेत्रफल = Δ PQR का क्षेत्रफल - (ΔPXZ का क्षेत्रफल + Δ QXY का क्षेत्रफल)

\n\n

⇒ XYRZ का क्षेत्रफल = 30 - (5 + 6) = 30 - 11 = 19 इकाई2

\n\n

अब,

\n\n

XYRZ का क्षेत्रफल : Δ PXZ का क्षेत्रफल = 19 : 5 = 19/5

\n\n

अतः, सही उत्तर विकल्प (2) है।

\n\n

\n\n

\\(\\rm\\frac{\\text { Area of } \\triangle P X Z}{\\text { Area of } \\triangle P Q R}=\\frac{\\frac{1}{2} × P X . P Z \\cdot \\sin P}{\\frac{1}{2} × P Q . P R \\cdot \\sin P}\\) = \\(\\rm\\left(\\frac{P X}{P Q} × \\frac{P Z}{P R}\\right)=\\left(\\frac{1}{2} × \\frac{1}{3}\\right)\\)

\n\n

\n\n

PX ∶ XQ = 1 ∶ 1, PZ ∶ ZR = 1 ∶ 2, QY ∶ YR = 2 ∶ 3

\n\n

অনুসৃত ধারণা:

\n\n

Δ ABC-তে, Δ ABC এর ক্ষেত্রফল = (1/2)AB × AC × SinA

\n\n

গণনা:

\n\n

\n\n

ধাপ 1: PXZ : PQR এর ক্ষেত্রফলের অনুপাত নির্ণয় করো।

\n\n

\\(\\rm\\frac{\\text { Area of } \\triangle P X Z}{\\text { Area of } \\triangle P Q R}=\\frac{\\frac{1}{2} × P X . P Z \\cdot \\sin P}{\\frac{1}{2} × P Q . P R \\cdot \\sin P}\\) = \\(\\rm\\left(\\frac{P X}{P Q} × \\frac{P Z}{P R}\\right)=\\left(\\frac{1}{2} × \\frac{1}{3}\\right)\\)

\n\n

⇒ (Δ PXZ)এর ক্ষেত্রফল / ( Δ PQR) এর ক্ষেত্রফল = 1/6 ---(1)

\n\n

(Δ PXZ) এর ক্ষেত্রফল / (Δ PQR) এর ক্ষেত্রফল = 1/6 × 5/5 = 5/30

\n\n

\n\n

এখানে, উভয় ক্ষেত্রেই Δ PQR এর ক্ষেত্রফল 30 বর্গ একক।

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

ಹಂತ 1: PXZ : PQR ನ ವಿಸ್ತೀರ್ಣದ ಅನುಪಾತವನ್ನು ಕಂಡುಹಿಡಿಯಿರಿ.

\n\n

\\(\\rm\\frac{\\text { Area of } \\triangle P X Z}{\\text { Area of } \\triangle P Q R}=\\frac{\\frac{1}{2} × P X . P", "kn": "\n

ನೀಡಲಾಗಿದೆ:

\n\n

PX ∶ XQ = 1 ∶ 1, PZ ∶ ZR = 1 ∶ 2, QY ∶ YR = 2 ∶ 3.

\n\n

ಬಳಸಿದ ಪರಿಕಲ್ಪನೆ:

\n\n

Δ ABC ಯಲ್ಲಿ, Δ ABC ಯ ಪ್ರದೇಶ = (1/2)AB × AC × SinA

\n\n

ಲೆಕ್ಕಾಚಾರ:

\n\n

\n\n

ಹಂತ 1: PXZ : PQR ನ ವಿಸ್ತೀರ್ಣದ ಅನುಪಾತವನ್ನು ಕಂಡುಹಿಡಿಯಿರಿ.

\n\n

\\(\\rm\\frac{\\text { Area of } \\triangle P X Z}{\\text { Area of } \\triangle P Q R}=\\frac{\\frac{1}{2} × P X . P Z \\cdot \\sin P}{\\frac{1}{2} × P Q . P R \\cdot \\sin P}\\) = \\(\\rm\\left(\\frac{P X}{P Q} × \\frac{P Z}{P R}\\right)=\\left(\\frac{1}{2} × \\frac{1}{3}\\right)\\)

\n\n

⇒ ar (Δ PXZ) / ar ( Δ PQR) = 1/6 ---(1)

\n\n

ಹಂತ 2: QXY : PQR ನ ವಿಸ್ತೀರ್ಣದ ಅನುಪಾತವನ್ನು ಕಂಡುಹಿಡಿಯಿರಿ.

\n\n

\\(\\rm\\frac{\\text { Area of } \\triangle Q X Y}{\\text { Area of } \\triangle P Q R}=\\frac{\\frac{1}{2} Q X \\cdot Q Y \\cdot \\sin Q}{\\frac{1}{2} \\cdot P Q \\cdot Q R \\cdot \\sin Q}\\) = \\(\\rm\\frac{Q X}{P Q} × \\frac{Q Y}{Q R}=\\frac{1}{2} × \\frac{2}{5}\\)

\n\n

⇒ ar (Δ QXY) / ar ( Δ PQR) = 1/5 ---(2)

\n\n

ಈಗ, ಎರಡೂ ಸಮೀಕರಣಗಳಲ್ಲಿ Δ PQR ನ ವಿಸ್ತೀರ್ಣವನ್ನು ಸಮೀಕರಿಸಿ,

\n\n

ar (Δ PXZ) / ar (Δ PQR) = 1/6 × 5/5 = 5/30

\n\n

ar (Δ QXY) / ar (Δ PQR) = 1/5 × 6/6 = 6/30

\n\n

ಇಲ್ಲಿ, ಎರಡೂ ಸಂದರ್ಭಗಳಲ್ಲಿ Δ PQR ನ ವಿಸ್ತೀರ್ಣ 30 ಚದರ ಯೂನಿಟ್‌ಗಳು.

\n\n

ಆದ್ದರಿಂದ, Δ PXZ ನ ವಿಸ್ತೀರ್ಣ = 5 ಘಟಕ 2

\n\n

ಮತ್ತು, Δ QXY = 6 ಘಟಕ 2 ರ ವಿಸ್ತೀರ್ಣ

\n\n

ಆದ್ದರಿಂದ, XYRZ ನ ವಿಸ್ತೀರ್ಣ = Δ PQR ನ ವಿಸ್ತೀರ್ಣ - (ΔPXZ ನ ವಿಸ್ತೀರ್ಣ + Δ QXY ನ ವಿಸ್ತೀರ್ಣ)

\n\n

XYRZ ನ ⇒ ಅಂಶಗಳು = 30 - (5 + 6) = 30 - 11 = 19 ಘಟಕ 2

\n\n

ಈಗ,

\n\n

XYRZ ನ ವಿಸ್ತೀರ್ಣ : Δ PXZ ನ ರಿಯಾ = 19 : 5 = 19/5

\n\n

ಆದ್ದರಿಂದ, ಸರಿಯಾದ ಉತ್ತರವು ಆಯ್ಕೆ (2) ಆಗಿದೆ.

Shortcut Trick\n

New Quotient = (New Dividend % ÷ New Divisor %) × Original Quotient

\n

New Quotient = [(100% − 10%) ÷ (100% − 20%)] × 24

\n

New Quotient = (90% ÷ 80%) × 24

\n

New Quotient = (9 ÷ 8) × 24 = 27

{"prompt": "4", "value": "30"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}}, "correct_option": "1", "solution_html": {"en": "\n

Shortcut Trick\n

New Quotient = (New Dividend % ÷ New Divisor %) × Original Quotient

\n

New Quotient = [(100% − 10%) ÷ (100% − 20%)] × 24

\n

New Quotient = (90% ÷ 80%) × 24

\n

New Quotient = (9 ÷ 8) × 24 = 27

\n

∴ The correct answer is 27.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n\n\n\n

Given:

\n

Original Quotient = 24

\n

Original Remainder = 0

\n

New Dividend is 10% less than the Original Dividend.

\n

New Divisor is 20% less than the Original Divisor.

\n\n

Formula Used:

\n

Dividend = (Divisor × Quotient) + Remainder

\n\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ Let the Original Dividend be D and the Original Divisor be d.

\n

⇒ D = (d × 24) + 0

\n

⇒ D ÷ d = 24

\n

⇒ New Dividend = D − (10% of D) = 0.9D

\n

⇒ New Divisor = d − (20% of d) = 0.8d

\n

⇒ New Quotient = New Dividend ÷ New Divisor

\n

⇒ New Quotient = 0.9D ÷ 0.8d

\n

⇒ New Quotient = (0.9 ÷ 0.8) × (D ÷ d)

\n

⇒ Substitute D ÷ d = 24:

\n

⇒ New Quotient = (9 ÷ 8) × 24

\n

⇒ New Quotient = 9 × 3 = 27

\n

∴ The correct answer is 27.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n\n

Division Algorithm

\n

The foundational rule of division states that Dividend = (Divisor × Quotient) + Remainder. When the remainder is exactly zero, the dividend is a perfect multiple of the divisor.

\n\n

Percentage Change in Fractions

\n

If the numerator of a fraction changes by x% and the denominator changes by y%, the resulting fraction becomes [(100 ± x) ÷ (100 ± y)] × Original Fraction.

\n\n

Base Change

\n

When any given quantity is decreased by R%, the remaining quantity is computed directly as (100 − R)% of its original value. For example, a 10% decrease perfectly leaves 90% of the original value.

दिया गया:

\n\n

मूल भागफल (Q) = 24 शेष = 0 लाभांश 10% कम लिया गया भाजक 20% कम लिया गया

\n\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

\n\n

लाभांश = Q × भाजक

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

माना कि मूल लाभांश (D) 100 इकाई है।

\n\n

सही लाभांश (D) और भाजक (d) का परिणाम भागफल 24 और शेषफल 0 (यानी, D = 24d) होता है।

\n\n

सुहास ने ग़लती से लाभांश का 90% (0.9D) और भाजक का 80% (0.8d) ले लिया।

\n\n

इन दो गलत मानों का भागफल (Q) है:

\n\n

Q = 0.9D / 0.8d.

\n\n

स्थानापन्न D = 24d

\n\n

Q = 0.9 × 24d / 0.8d Q = 21.6 / 0.8 Q = 27.

\n\n

तो, सुहास को 27 का भागफल प्राप्त होगा।

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

ଦିଆଯାଇଥିବା:

\n\n

"ta": {"value": "ஒரு செவ்வக அறையின் பரப்பளவு 60 மீ2 மற்றும் சுற்றளவு 34 மீ. செவ்வக அறையின் மூலைவிட்டத்தின் நீளம் ஒரு சதுரத்தின் பக்கத்தைப் போன்றது. சதுரத்தின் பரப்பளவு (மீ2 இல்) என்ன ?", "options": [{"prompt": "1", "value": "169"}, {"prompt": "2", "value": "144"}, {"prompt": "3", "value": "300"}, {"prompt": "4", "value": "244"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}}, "correct_option": "1", "solution_html": {"en": "\n

Shortcut Trick\n

L + B = Perimeter ÷ 2 = 34 ÷ 2 = 17 m

\n

Area of Rectangle = L × B = 60 m2

\n

Diagonal of Rectangle (D) = √(L2 + B2)

\n

Area of Square = D2 = L2 + B2 = (L + B)2 − 2 × L × B

\n

Area = 172 − 2 × 60 = 289 − 120 = 169 m2

\n

∴ The correct answer is 169 m2.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

Area of rectangular room = 60 m2

\n

Perimeter of rectangular room = 34 m

\n

Side of the square = Diagonal of the rectangular room

\n\n

Formula Used:

\n

Perimeter of rectangle = 2 × (L + B)

\n

Area of rectangle = L × B

\n

Diagonal of rectangle = √(L2 + B2)

\n

Area of square = (Side)2

\n\n\n\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ 2 × (L + B) = 34

\n

⇒ L + B = 17

\n

⇒ L × B = 60

\n

⇒ Diagonal of rectangle = √(L2 + B2)

\n

⇒ Side of square = √(L2 + B2)

\n

⇒ Area of square = L2 + B2

\n

⇒ We know that (L + B)2 = L2 + B2 + 2 × L × B

\n

⇒ L2 + B2 = 172 − 2 × 60

\n

⇒ Area of square = 289 − 120 = 169 m2

\n

∴ The correct answer is 169 m2.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

Algebraic Identities

\n

The expansion formula (a + b)2 = a2 + b2 + 2 × a × b is heavily utilized in geometry to directly deduce sum of squares or diagonals without having to find the precise values of the individual side lengths.

\n

Properties of a Rectangle

\n

Opposite sides are parallel and strictly equal in length. All interior angles are equal to 90°, and diagonals are equal in length and bisect each other.

\n

Properties of a Square

\n

All four sides are equal, making the area simply square of its side. Both diagonals are precisely equal, intersect perpendicularly at 90°, and have a total length of a√2.

प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

\n\n

आयत का क्षेत्रफल = a × b, जहाँ आयत की लंबाई और चौड़ाई क्रमश: a और b है। 

\n\n

आयत का परिमाप = 2(a + b)

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

ab = 60

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

a2 + b2 + 2ab = 289

\n\n

⇒ a2 + b2 + 2 × 60 = 289

\n\n

⇒ a2 + b2 = 169

\n\n

⇒ √a2 + b2 = आयत का विकर्ण = वर्ग की भुजा = √169

\n\n

⇒ वर्ग की भुजा = 13 सेमी

\n\n

वर्ग का क्षेत्रफल = 13 × 13 = 169 सेमी2

செவ்வகத்தின் பரப்பளவு = a × b, a மற்றும் b ஆகியவை முறையே செவ்வகத்தின் நீளம் மற்றும் அகலம் ஆகும்.

\n\n

செவ்வகத்தின் சுற்றளவு = 2(a + b)

\n\n

சதுரத்தின் பரப்பளவு = பக்கம்2

\n\n

கணக்கீடு:

\n\n

ab = 60

\n\n

2(a + b) = 34

\n\n

⇒ a + b = 17

\n\n

இருபுறமும் சதுரம்,

\n\n

a2 + b2 + 2ab = 289

\n\n

⇒ a2 + b2 + 2 × 60 = 289

\n\n

⇒ a2 + b2 = 169

\n\n

⇒ √ a2 + b2 = செவ்வகத்தின் மூலைவிட்டம் = சதுரத்தின் பக்கம் = √169

\n\n

⇒ சதுரத்தின் பக்கம் = 13 செ.மீ

\n\n

சதுரத்தின் பரப்பளவு = 13 × 13 = 169 செமீ2

Effective rate for CI at 10% p.a. compounded half-yearly for 1 year = 5% + 5% + (5×5)/100 = 10.25%

\n

Shortcut Trick\n

Ratio of corresponding sides = √(Ratio of areas) = √(25 / 144) = 5 / 12

\n

Since ΔABC ∼ ΔPQR, the corresponding side to AC is PR.

\n

Therefore, AC / PR = 5 / 12

\n

9 / PR = 5 / 12 ⇒ PR = (9 × 12) ÷ 5

\n

PR = 108 ÷ 5 = 21.6

\n

∴ The correct answer is 21.6 cm.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

ΔABC ∼ ΔPQR

\n

Area(ΔABC) / Area(ΔPQR) = 25 / 144

\n

AB = 12 cm, BC = 7 cm, AC = 9 cm

\n

Formula Used:

\n

When two triangles are similar, the ratio of their areas is equal to the square of the ratio of their corresponding sides:

\n

Area(ΔABC) / Area(ΔPQR) = (AC / PR)2

\n\n\n\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ (AC / PR)2 = 25 / 144

\n

⇒ AC / PR = √(25 / 144)

\n

⇒ AC / PR = 5 / 12

\n

⇒ Substitute the value of AC = 9 cm:

\n

⇒ 9 / PR = 5 / 12

\n

⇒ 5 × PR = 9 × 12

\n

⇒ 5 × PR = 108

\n

⇒ PR = 108 ÷ 5 = 21.6

\n

∴ The correct answer is 21.6 cm.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

Similarity of Triangles

\n

Two triangles are similar if their corresponding angles are equal and their corresponding sides are proportional. If ΔABC ∼ ΔPQR, then the ratio pattern strictly follows AB/PQ = BC/QR = AC/PR.

\n

Area Ratio Theorem

\n

The ratio of the areas of two similar triangles is equal to the square of the ratio of any pair of their corresponding sides, altitudes, or medians.

\n

Perimeter Ratio of Similar Triangles

\n

Unlike the area ratio, the ratio of the perimeters of two similar triangles is directly equal to the ratio of their corresponding sides without any squared values.

चूँकि ΔABC ∼ ΔPQR है, AC की संगत भुजा PR है।

\n

\n

PR = 108 ÷ 5 = 21.6

\n

Additional Information

\n

त्रिभुजों की समरूपता

\n

दो त्रिभुज समरूप होते हैं यदि उनके संगत कोण बराबर हों और उनकी संगत भुजाएँ समानुपाती हों। यदि ΔABC ∼ ΔPQR है, तो अनुपात पैटर्न पूर्णतः AB/PQ = BC/QR = AC/PR का अनुसरण करता है।

\n

क्षेत्रफल अनुपात प्रमेय

\n

दो समरूप त्रिभुजों के क्षेत्रफलों का अनुपात उनकी संगत भुजाओं, शीर्षलंबों या माध्यिकाओं के किसी भी युग्म के अनुपात के वर्ग के बराबर होता है।

\n

समरूप त्रिभुजों के परिमाप का अनुपात

\n

क्षेत्रफल के अनुपात के विपरीत, दो समरूप त्रिभुजों के परिमापों का अनुपात बिना किसी वर्ग मान के सीधे उनकी संगत भुजाओं के अनुपात के बराबर होता है।

पर्यायी पद्धत

दिले आहे:

\n

ΔABC ∼ ΔPQR

क्षेत्रफळ(ΔABC) / क्षेत्रफळ(ΔPQR) = 25 / 144

AB = 12 सेमी, BC = 7 सेमी, AC = 9 सेमी

⇒ AC / PR = 5 / 12

\n

⇒ 9 / PR = 5 / 12

⇒ 5 x PR = 9 x 12

\n

⇒ 5 x PR = 108

\n

⇒ PR = 108 ÷ 5 = 21.6

\n

∴ योग्य उत्तर 21.6 सेमी आहे.

अतिरिक्त माहिती

त्रिकोणांची समरूपता

\n

दोन त्रिकोण समरूप असतात जर त्यांचे संबंधित कोन समान असतील आणि त्यांच्या संबंधित बाजू समानुपातिक असतील. जर ΔABC ∼ ΔPQR असेल, तर गुणोत्तर नमुना AB/PQ = BC/QR = AC/PR याप्रमाणे कठोरपणे पाळला जातो.

\n

क्षेत्रफळ गुणोत्तर प्रमेय

\n

दोन समरूप त्रिकोणांच्या क्षेत्रफळांचे गुणोत्तर त्यांच्या संबंधित बाजू, उंची किंवा मध्यगेच्या कोणत्याही जोडीच्या गुणोत्तराच्या वर्गाइतके असते.

समरूप त्रिकोणांचे परिमिती गुणोत्तर

\n

क्षेत्रफळ गुणोत्तराप्रमाणे नसून, दोन समरूप त्रिकोणांच्या परिमितीचे गुणोत्तर त्यांच्या संबंधित बाजूंच्या गुणोत्तराच्या थेट समान असते, कोणत्याही वर्ग मूल्यांशिवाय.

ধারণা:

\n\n

অনুরূপ ত্রিভুজগুলির ক্ষেত্রফল তাদের নিজ নিজ বাহুর বর্গের সমানুপাতিক।

⇒ PR = 9 × \\(12\\over5\\) = 21.6 সেমি

∴ PR এর মান 21.6 সেমি।

\n\n43:T19a4,\n

શોર્ટકટ ટ્રીક\n

અનુરૂપ બાજુઓનો ગુણોત્તર = √(ક્ષેત્રફળનો ગુણોત્તર) = √(25 / 144) = 5 / 12

\n

આપેલ છે કે ΔABC ∼ ΔPQR, AC ને અનુરૂપ બાજુ PR છે.

\n

તેથી, AC / PR = 5 / 12

\n

9 / PR = 5 / 12 ⇒ PR = (9 x 12) ÷ 5

\n

PR = 108 ÷ 5 = 21.6

\n

∴ સાચો જવાબ 21.6 cm છે.

\n\n

વૈકલ્પિક પદ્ધતિ

\n

આપેલ છે:

\n

ΔABC ∼ ΔPQR

\n

ક્ષેત્રફળ(ΔABC) / ક્ષેત્રફળ(ΔPQR) = 25 / 144

\n

AB = 12 cm, BC = 7 cm, AC = 9 cm

\n

ઉપયોગમાં લેવાયેલ સૂત્ર:

\n

જ્યારે બે ત્રિકોણ સમરૂપ હોય, ત્યારે તેમના ક્ષેત્રફળનો ગુણોત્તર તેમની અનુરૂપ બાજુઓના ગુણોત્તરના વર્ગ બરાબર હોય છે:

\n

ક્ષેત્રફળ(ΔABC) / ક્ષેત્રફળ(ΔPQR) = (AC / PR)2

\n\n\n\n

ગણતરીઓ:

\n

⇒ (AC / PR)2 = 25 / 144

\n

⇒ AC / PR = √(25 / 144)

\n

⇒ AC / PR = 5 / 12

\n

⇒ AC = 9 cm ની કિંમત મૂકો:

\n

⇒ 9 / PR = 5 / 12

\n

⇒ 5 x PR = 9 x 12

\n

⇒ 5 x PR = 108

\n

⇒ PR = 108 ÷ 5 = 21.6

\n

∴ સાચો જવાબ 21.6 cm છે.

\n\n

વધારાની માહિતી

\n

ત્રિકોણની સમરૂપતા

\n

જો બે ત્રિકોણ સમરૂપ હોય તો તેમના અનુરૂપ ખૂણાઓ સમાન હોય છે અને તેમની અનુરૂપ બાજુઓ પ્રમાણસર હોય છે. જો ΔABC ∼ ΔPQR, તો ગુણોત્તર પેટર્ન સખત રીતે AB/PQ = BC/QR = AC/PR નું પાલન કરે છે.

\n

ક્ષેત્રફળ ગુણોત્તર પ્રમેય

\n

બે સમરૂપ ત્રિકોણના ક્ષેત્રફળનો ગુણોત્તર તેમની અનુરૂપ બાજુઓ, ઊંચાઈઓ અથવા મધ્યગાઓની કોઈપણ જોડીના ગુણોત્તરના વર્ગ બરાબર હોય છે.

\n

સમરૂપ ત્રિકોણના પરિમિતિનો ગુણોત્તર

\n

ક્ષેત્રફળ ગુણોત્તરથી વિપરીત, બે સમરૂપ ત્રિકોણની પરિમિતિનો ગુણોત્તર કોઈપણ વર્ગમૂળ વિના તેમની અનુરૂપ બાજુઓના ગુણોત્તર બરાબર હોય છે.

Shortcut Trick\n

ಅನುರೂಪ ಬದಿಗಳ ಅನುಪಾತ = √(ವಿಸ್ತೀರ್ಣಗಳ ಅನುಪಾತ) = √(25 / 144) = 5 / 12

\n

ΔABC ~ ΔPQR ಆಗಿರುವುದರಿಂದ, AC ಗೆ ಅನುರೂಪವಾದ ಬದಿ PR ಆಗಿದೆ.

\n

ಆದ್ದರಿಂದ, AC / PR = 5 / 12

\n

9 / PR = 5 / 12 ⇒ PR = (9 x 12) ÷ 5

\n

PR = 108 ÷ 5 = 21.6

\n

∴ ಸರಿಯಾದ ಉತ್ತರ 21.6 cm.

\n\n

ತ್ರಿಭುಜಗಳ ಸಮರೂಪತೆ

ಎರಡು ತ್ರಿಭುಜಗಳು ಸಮರೂಪವಾಗಿದ್ದರೆ, ಅವುಗಳ ಅನುರೂಪ ಕೋನಗಳು ಸಮಾನವಾಗಿರುತ್ತದೆ ಮತ್ತು ಅವುಗಳ ಅನುರೂಪ ಬದಿಗಳು ಅನುಪಾತದಲ್ಲಿರುತ್ತದೆ. ΔABC ~ ΔPQR ಆಗಿದ್ದರೆ, ಅನುಪಾತದ ಕ್ರಮವು AB/PQ = BC/QR = AC/PR ಅನ್ನು ಕಟ್ಟುನಿಟ್ಟಾಗಿ ಅನುಸರಿಸುತ್ತದೆ.

Shortcut Trick

\n

ஒத்த பக்கங்களின் விகிதம் = √(பரப்பளவுகளின் விகிதம்) = √(25 / 144) = 5 / 12

\n

ΔABC ∼ ΔPQR என்பதால், AC க்கு ஒத்த பக்கம் PR ஆகும்.

\n

எனவே, AC / PR = 5 / 12

\n

9 / PR = 5 / 12 ⇒ PR = (9 × 12) ÷ 5

\n

PR = 108 ÷ 5 = 21.6

\n

∴ சரியான பதில் 21.6 செ.மீ.

\n

கொடுக்கப்பட்டது:

\n

ΔABC ∼ ΔPQR

\n

பரப்பளவு (ΔABC) / பரப்பளவு (ΔPQR) = 25 / 144

\n

AB = 12 செ.மீ, BC = 7 செ.மீ, AC = 9 செ.மீ

\n

பரப்பளவு (ΔABC) / பரப்பளவு (ΔPQR) = (AC / PR)2

\n

Additional Information

\n

Similarity of Triangles

\n

இரண்டு முக்கோணங்களின் ஒத்த கோணங்கள் சமமாக இருந்தால் மற்றும் அவற்றின் ஒத்த பக்கங்கள் விகிதாசாரமாக இருந்தால் அவை ஒத்தவை. ΔABC ∼ ΔPQR எனில், விகித முறை AB/PQ = BC/QR = AC/PR என்பதை கண்டிப்பாகப் பின்பற்றுகிறது.

\n

Area Ratio Theorem

\n

இரண்டு ஒத்த முக்கோணங்களின் பரப்பளவுகளின் விகிதம், அவற்றின் ஒத்த பக்கங்கள், உயரங்கள் அல்லது மையக் கோடுகளின் எந்தவொரு ஜோடியின் விகிதத்தின் வர்க்கத்திற்குச் சமம்.

\n

Perimeter Ratio of Similar Triangles

\n

பரப்பளவு விகிதத்தைப் போலன்றி, இரண்டு ஒத்த முக்கோணங்களின் சுற்றளவுகளின் விகிதம் எந்த வர்க்க மதிப்புகளும் இல்லாமல் அவற்றின் ஒத்த பக்கங்களின் விகிதத்திற்கு நேரடியாகச் சமம்.

\n46:T19b2,\n

Shortcut Trick\n

ଅନୁରୂପ ପାର୍ଶ୍ୱର ଅନୁପାତ = √(କ୍ଷେତ୍ରଫଳର ଅନୁପାତ) = √(25 / 144) = 5 / 12

\n

ଯେହେତୁ ΔABC ∼ ΔPQR, AC ର ଅନୁରୂପ ପାର୍ଶ୍ୱ ହେଉଛି PR।

\n

\n

PR = 108 ÷ 5 = 21.6

\n

∴ ସଠିକ୍ ଉତ୍ତର ହେଉଛି 21.6 cm।

ବ୍ୟବହୃତ ସୂତ୍ର:

\n

ଯେତେବେଳେ ଦୁଇଟି ତ୍ରିଭୁଜ ସମାନ ହୁଅନ୍ତି, ସେମାନଙ୍କ କ୍ଷେତ୍ରଫଳର ଅନୁପାତ ସେମାନଙ୍କ ଅନୁରୂପ ପାର୍ଶ୍ୱଗୁଡ଼ିକର ଅନୁପାତର ବର୍ଗ ସହିତ ସମାନ:

\n

କ୍ଷେତ୍ରଫଳ(ΔABC) / କ୍ଷେତ୍ରଫଳ(ΔPQR) = (AC / PR)2

\n\n\n\n

ଗଣନା:

\n

⇒ (AC / PR)2 = 25 / 144

\n

⇒ AC / PR = √(25 / 144)

\n

⇒ AC / PR = 5 / 12

\n

⇒ AC = 9 cm ର ମୂଲ୍ୟ ପ୍ରତିସ୍ଥାପନ କରନ୍ତୁ:

\n

⇒ 9 / PR = 5 / 12

\n

⇒ 5 x PR = 9 x 12

\n

⇒ 5 x PR = 108

\n

⇒ PR = 108 ÷ 5 = 21.6

\n

∴ ସଠିକ୍ ଉତ୍ତର ହେଉଛି 21.6 cm।

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

ତ୍ରିଭୁଜର ସମାନତା

\n

ଦୁଇଟି ତ୍ରିଭୁଜ ସମାନ ହୁଅନ୍ତି ଯଦି ସେମାନଙ୍କର ଅନୁରୂପ କୋଣଗୁଡ଼ିକ ସମାନ ଏବଂ ସେମାନଙ୍କର ଅନୁରୂପ ପାର୍ଶ୍ୱଗୁଡ଼ିକ ସମାନୁପାତିକ ହୁଏ। ଯଦି ΔABC ∼ ΔPQR, ତେବେ ଅନୁପାତ ଶୈଳୀ AB/PQ = BC/QR = AC/PR କୁ କଠୋର ଭାବରେ ଅନୁସରଣ କରେ।

\n

କ୍ଷେତ୍ରଫଳ ଅନୁପାତ ଉପପାଦ୍ୟ

\n

ଦୁଇଟି ସମାନ ତ୍ରିଭୁଜର କ୍ଷେତ୍ରଫଳର ଅନୁପାତ ସେମାନଙ୍କ ଅନୁରୂପ ପାର୍ଶ୍ୱ, ଉଚ୍ଚତା କିମ୍ବା ମାଧ୍ୟମିକାର ଯେକୌଣସି ଯୁଗଳର ଅନୁପାତର ବର୍ଗ ସହିତ ସମାନ।

\n

ସମାନ ତ୍ରିଭୁଜର ପରିସୀମା ଅନୁପାତ

\n

କ୍ଷେତ୍ରଫଳ ଅନୁପାତ ପରି, ଦୁଇଟି ସମାନ ତ୍ରିଭୁଜର ପରିସୀମାର ଅନୁପାତ ସେମାନଙ୍କ ଅନୁରୂପ ପାର୍ଶ୍ୱଗୁଡ଼ିକର ଅନୁପାତ ସହିତ ସିଧାସଳଖ ସମାନ, ବିନା କୌଣସି ବର୍ଗ ମୂଲ୍ୟରେ।

\n47:T19c8,\n

ਸੰਖੇਪ ਤਰੀਕਾ\n

ਅਨੁਸਾਰੀ ਭੁਜਾਵਾਂ ਦਾ ਅਨੁਪਾਤ = √(ਖੇਤਰਫਲਾਂ ਦਾ ਅਨੁਪਾਤ) = √(25 / 144) = 5 / 12

\n

ਕਿਉਂਕਿ ΔABC ∼ ΔPQR, AC ਦੇ ਅਨੁਸਾਰੀ ਭੁਜਾ PR ਹੈ।

\n

ਇਸ ਲਈ, AC / PR = 5 / 12

\n

9 / PR = 5 / 12 ⇒ PR = (9 x 12) ÷ 5

\n

PR = 108 ÷ 5 = 21.6

\n

∴ ਸਹੀ ਉੱਤਰ 21.6 cm ਹੈ।

\n\n

ਵਿਕਲਪਕ ਵਿਧੀ

\n

ਦਿੱਤਾ ਗਿਆ ਹੈ:

\n

ΔABC ∼ ΔPQR

\n

ਖੇਤਰਫਲ(ΔABC) / ਖੇਤਰਫਲ(ΔPQR) = 25 / 144

\n

AB = 12 cm, BC = 7 cm, AC = 9 cm

\n

ਵਰਤਿਆ ਗਿਆ ਫਾਰਮੂਲਾ:

\n

ਜਦੋਂ ਦੋ ਤ੍ਰਿਕੋਣ ਸਮਰੂਪ ਹੁੰਦੇ ਹਨ, ਤਾਂ ਉਹਨਾਂ ਦੇ ਖੇਤਰਫਲਾਂ ਦਾ ਅਨੁਪਾਤ ਉਹਨਾਂ ਦੀਆਂ ਅਨੁਸਾਰੀ ਭੁਜਾਵਾਂ ਦੇ ਅਨੁਪਾਤ ਦੇ ਵਰਗ ਦੇ ਬਰਾਬਰ ਹੁੰਦਾ ਹੈ:

\n

ਖੇਤਰਫਲ(ΔABC) / ਖੇਤਰਫਲ(ΔPQR) = (AC / PR)2

\n\n ਸੰਖੇਪ ਤਰੀਕਾ\n

ਅਨੁਸਾਰੀ ਭੁਜਾਵਾਂ ਦਾ ਅਨੁਪਾਤ = √(ਖੇਤਰਫਲਾਂ ਦਾ ਅਨੁਪਾਤ) = √(25 / 144) = 5 / 12

ਵਾਧੂ ਜਾਣਕਾਰੀ

\n

ਤ੍ਰਿਕੋਣਾਂ ਦੀ ਸਮਰੂਪਤਾ

\n

ਦੋ ਤ੍ਰਿਕੋਣ ਸਮਰੂਪ ਹੁੰਦੇ ਹਨ ਜੇ ਉਹਨਾਂ ਦੇ ਅਨੁਸਾਰੀ ਕੋਣ ਬਰਾਬਰ ਹੁੰਦੇ ਹਨ ਅਤੇ ਉਹਨਾਂ ਦੀਆਂ ਅਨੁਸਾਰੀ ਭੁਜਾਵਾਂ ਅਨੁਪਾਤਕ ਹੁੰਦੀਆਂ ਹਨ। ਜੇ ΔABC ∼ ΔPQR, ਤਾਂ ਅਨੁਪਾਤ ਪੈਟਰਨ ਸਖਤੀ ਨਾਲ AB/PQ = BC/QR = AC/PR ਦੀ ਪਾਲਣਾ ਕਰਦਾ ਹੈ।

\n

ਸਮਰੂਪ ਤ੍ਰਿਕੋਣਾਂ ਦਾ ਘੇਰਾ ਅਨੁਪਾਤ

\n

ਖੇਤਰਫਲ ਅਨੁਪਾਤ ਦੇ ਉਲਟ, ਦੋ ਸਮਰੂਪ ਤ੍ਰਿਕੋਣਾਂ ਦੇ ਘੇਰਿਆਂ ਦਾ ਅਨੁਪਾਤ ਕਿਸੇ ਵੀ ਵਰਗ ਦੇ ਮੁੱਲ ਤੋਂ ਬਿਨਾਂ ਉਹਨਾਂ ਦੀਆਂ ਅਨੁਸਾਰੀ ਭੁਜਾਵਾਂ ਦੇ ਅਨੁਪਾਤ ਦੇ ਸਿੱਧੇ ਬਰਾਬਰ ਹੁੰਦਾ ਹੈ।

\n48:T142f,\n

Shortcut Trick\n

Effective interest in 1st case = 7.5% × (10/3) = 25% → Amount = 125% of Principal.

\n

Effective interest in 2nd case = 8% × (15/4) = 30% → Amount = 130% of Principal.

\n

Since 125% of Principal = ₹4900

\n

130% of Principal = (4900 ÷ 125) × 130 = 39.2 × 130 = ₹5096.

\n

∴ The correct answer is ₹5096.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n\n

\n

Amount in 1st case (A1) = ₹4900

\n

Rate (R1) = 7.5% p.a., Time (T1) = 3 years 4 months = 3 + 1/3 = 10/3 years

\n

Rate (R2) = 8% p.a., Time (T2) = 3 + 3/4 = 15/4 years

\n\n

Formula Used:

\n

Simple Interest (SI) = (P × R × T) ÷ 100

\n

Amount in 1st case (A1) = ₹4900

\n

Rate (R1) = 7.5% p.a., Time (T1) = 3 years 4 months = 3 + 1/3 = 10/3 years

\n

Rate (R2) = 8% p.a., Time (T2) = 3 + 3/4 = 15/4 years

\n\n

Formula Used:

\n

Simple Interest (SI) = (P × R × T) ÷ 100

\n

Amount (A) = P + SI = P × (1 + RT/100)

\n\n\n\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ In the 1st case, SI1% = 7.5 × (10/3) = 25%

\n

⇒ Amount (A1) = 100% + 25% = 125% of P

\n

⇒ 125% of P = ₹4900

\n

⇒ P = (4900 × 100) ÷ 125 = ₹3920

\n

⇒ In the 2nd case, SI2% = 8 × (15/4) = 30%

\n

⇒ Amount (A2) = 100% + 30% = 130% of P

\n

⇒ A2 = 130% of 3920 = (130 ÷ 100) × 3920

\n

⇒ A2 = 1.3 × 3920 = ₹5096

\n

∴ The correct answer is ₹5096.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n\n

Effective Interest Rate in SI

\n

In Simple Interest, the effective interest percentage for a given period is simply the product of the rate and time (R × T)%. This allows for direct calculation without finding the principal every time.

\n\n

Amount Formula for Simple Interest

\n

Amount (A) = P + SI = P(1 + RT/100). The principal remains constant throughout the entire period, unlike compound interest where interest is added to the principal.

\n\n

Simple vs Compound Interest

\n

For the first year, both simple and compound interests are identical (if compounded annually). From the second year onwards, CI will be greater than SI because interest is earned on accumulated interest.

दिया गया है:

\n\n

मिश्रधन = 4,900 रुपये

\n\n

ब्याज की दर = 7.5% वार्षिक साधारण ब्याज

\n\n

समय = 3 वर्ष 4 महीने = 10/3 वर्ष

\n\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

\n\n

साधारण व्याज = \\(\\frac{P × R × T}{100}\\)

\n\n

मिश्रधन = मूलधन + साधारण ब्याज

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

मान लीजिए राशि 100x है।

\n\n

साधारण ब्याज = \\(\\frac{100x × 7.5 × 10}{100 × 3}\\) = 75x/3

\n\n

साधारण ब्याज = 25x

\n\n

∴ मिश्रधन = 100x + 25x = 125x

\n\n

⇒ 125x = 4900

\n\n

⇒ 5x = 196

\n\n

⇒ 100x = 3920

\n\n

∴ राशि  = 3920 रुपये

\n\n

अब 8% की दर से और 15/4 वर्षों के लिए 3920 रुपये पर साधारण ब्याज

\n\n

⇒ साधारण ब्याज = \\(\\frac{3920 × 8 × 15}{100 × 4}\\) = 392 × 3 = 1176

\n\n

मिश्रधन = 3920 + 1176 = 5096

\n\n

∴ अभीष्ट मिश्रधन 5096 रुपये है।

\n\n

Shortcut Trickसाधारण ब्याज की कुल दर = समय × दर - इस सूत्र का उपयोग करने पर,

\n\n

साधारण ब्याज1 = 7.5 × (10/3) = 25%

\n\n

मिश्रधन1 = 125%

\n\n

और, साधारण ब्याज2 = 8 × (15/4) = 30%

\n\n

मिश्रधन2 = 130%

\n\n

चूँकि, मिश्रधन1 = 4900

\n\n

⇒ 125% → 4900

\n\n

⇒ 130% → 5096

\n\n

⇒ मिश्रधन2 = 5096 रुपये

\n\n

∴ अभीष्ट मिश्रधन 5096 रुपये है।

ଦିଆଯାଇଥିବା:

\n\n

ସମୁଳ ସୁଧ =  4,900 ଟଙ୍କା

\n\n

ସୁଧ ହାର = 7.5% ବାର୍ଷିକ  ସରଳ ସୁଧ 

\n\n

ସମୟ = 3 ବର୍ଷ 4 ମାସ = 10/3 ବର୍ଷ 

\n\n

ବ୍ୟବହୃତ ସୂତ୍ର:

\n\n

Shortcut Trick\n

The radius, tangent, and the line joining the centre to the external point form a right-angled triangle.

\n

Radius (r) = √(Distance2 − Tangent2)

\n

⇒ r = √(102 − 82) = √(100 − 64) = √36 = 6 cm.

\n

Area of circle = π × r2 = π × 62 = 36π cm2.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 36π cm2.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

Distance of point P from the centre (OP) = 10 cm

\n

Length of the tangent (PT) = 8 cm

\n\n

Formula Used:

\n

Pythagoras Theorem: Hypotenuse2 = Base2 + Perpendicular2

\n

Area of a circle = πr2

\n

Calculations:

\n

We know that the radius is always perpendicular to the tangent at the point of contact.

\n

⇒ ∠OTP = 90°

\n

In right-angled ΔOTP, applying Pythagoras theorem:

\n

⇒ OP2 = OT2 + PT2

\n

⇒ 102 = r2 + 82

\n

⇒ 100 = r2 + 64

\n

⇒ r2 = 100 − 64 = 36

\n

⇒ r = 6 cm

\n

Now, finding the area of the circle:

\n

⇒ Area = π × r2

\n

⇒ Area = π × 62

\n

⇒ Area = 36π cm2

\n

∴ The correct answer is 36π cm2.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

Radius-Tangent Theorem

\n

A tangent to a circle is always perpendicular to the radius drawn at the exact point of tangency.

\n

Tangents from External Point

\n

The lengths of the two tangents drawn from a single external point to a circle are perfectly equal in measure.

\n

Secant-Tangent Rule

\n

If a tangent and a secant are drawn from an external point, then Tangent2 = External Secant Segment × Total Secant Length.

वर्तुळाच्या स्पर्शिकेची (PT) लांबी 8 सेमी आहे.

\n\n

वापरलेली संकल्पना:

\n\n

वर्तुळाच्या कोणत्याही बिंदूवरील स्पर्शिका संपर्क बिंदूद्वारे त्रिज्याला लंब असते.

\n\n

\n\n

∴ OP2 = PT2 + OT2 

\n\n

वर्तुळाचे क्षेत्रफळ = π R2

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

O हे दिलेल्या वर्तुळाचे केंद्र आहे असे समजा.

\n\n

तर, OP = 10 सेमी आणि PT = 8 सेमी

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

একটি বৃত্তের যে কোনও বিন্দুতে স্পর্শকটি সংযোগ বিন্দুর মাধ্যমে ব্যাসার্ধের জন্য লম্ব হয়।

\n\n

\n\n

∴ OP2 = PT2 + OT2 

\n\n

একটি বৃত্তের অঞ্চল = π R2

\n\n

গণনা:

\n\n

ধরি প্রদত্ত বৃত্তের কেন্দ্র হল O 

\n\n

তারপরে, OP = 10 সেমি এবং PT = 8 সেমি

\n\n

এখন,

\n\n

OP2 = PT2 + OT2

\n\n

⇒ 102 = 82 + OT2

\n\n

⇒ OT2 = 100 - 64

\n\n

⇒ OT2 = 36

\n\n

⇒ OT = 6 সেমি

\n\n

⇒ 36π

\n\n

In the diagram given below area of Δ PQR is 36. What is the area of the Δ SQR:

नीचे दिये गए रेखाचित्र में Δ PQR का क्षेत्रफल = 36 है, तो Δ SQR का क्षेत्रफल कितना होगा?

Shortcut Trick\n

Triangles sharing the same vertex and having their bases on the same straight line have areas proportional to their base lengths.

\n

From the diagram dimensions, the ratio of the base segment SR to the total base PR is 5 : 6.

\n

Area of Δ SQR = (SR ÷ PR) × Area of Δ PQR

\n

Area of Δ SQR = (5 ÷ 6) × 36 = 30

\n

∴ The correct answer is 30.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

Area of Δ PQR = 36

\n

Point S lies on the side PR of Δ PQR.

\n

Based on the given figure, the ratio of the base length SR to the total base PR is 5 : 6.

\n

Concept Used:

\n

When two triangles share the same perpendicular height, the ratio of their areas is equal to the ratio of their respective bases.

\n\nप्रयुक्त सूत्र:

\n\n

पाइथागोरस प्रमेय:

\n\n

कर्ण 2 = आधार2 + लंब2

\n\n

त्रिभुज का क्षेत्रफल = \\(\\frac{1}{2}\\) आधार × ऊँचाई

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

\n\n

ΔPQS में, पाइथागोरस प्रमेय का प्रयोग करने पर,

\n\n
PQ2 + PS2 = QS2
\n\n
 
\n\n
PS2  = QS2 - PQ2 = 52 - 42
\n\n
 
\n\n
PS = √9 = 3 इकाई 
\n\n

इसलिए,

\n\n

ΔSQR का क्षेत्रफल = 36 - ΔPQS का क्षेत्रफल

\n\n

A = 36 - \\(\\frac{1}{2}\\) × 3 × 4 = 36 - 6

\n\n

A = 30 वर्ग इकाई

\n\n

∴ सही उत्तर 30 है।

\n\n73:Tcd2,\n

Shortcut TrickA field roller, in the shape of a cylinder, has a diameter of 1 m and length of 1¼ m. if the speed at which the roller rolls is 14 revolutions per minute, then the maximum area (in m2) that roll in 1 hour ", "options": [{"prompt": "1", "value": "<span style="">3560</span>"}, {"prompt": "2", "value": "<span style="">3300</span>"}, {"prompt": "3", "value": "<span style="">3600</span>"}, {"prompt": "4", "value": "<span style="">3960</span>"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}, "hn": {"value": "एक फील्ड रोलर, एक बेलन के आकार में, 1 मीटर का व्यास और 1¼ मीटर की लंबाई है।यदि रोलर 14 घूर्णन प्रति मिनट की गति से घूमता है, तो अधिकतम क्षेत्रफल (m2 में) जो यह 1 घंटे में रोल कर सकता है:34:Te42,\n

Shortcut Trick\n

Area rolled in 1 revolution = Curved Surface Area = πdh

\n

Area per revolution = 22/7 × 1 × 5/4 = 55/14 m2

\n

Total revolutions in 1 hour (60 min) = 14 × 60 = 840

\n

Total area = Total revolutions × Area per revolution

\n

Total area = 840 × (55/14) = 60 × 55 = 3300 m2

\n

∴ The correct answer is 3300 m2.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

Diameter of the roller (d) = 1 m

\n

Length of the roller (h) = 1¼ m = 5/4 m

\n

Speed = 14 revolutions per minute

\n

Time = 1 hour = 60 minutes

\n\n\n\n

Formula Used:

\n

Curved Surface Area (CSA) of a cylinder = π × d × h

\n

Total Area Rolled = Total Revolutions × CSA

\n\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ Total revolutions in 1 hour = 14 revolutions/minute × 60 minutes = 840 revolutions

\n

⇒ Area covered in 1 revolution (CSA) = (22/7) × 1 × (5/4)

\n

⇒ Area covered in 1 revolution = 110/28 = 55/14 m2

\n

⇒ Total area rolled in 1 hour = 840 × (55/14)

\n

⇒ Total area = (840 ÷ 14) × 55

\n

⇒ Total area = 60 × 55

\n

⇒ Total area = 3300 m2

\n

∴ The correct answer is 3300 m2.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

Cylinder Properties

\n

Volume = πr2h | Total Surface Area = 2πr(r + h) | Curved Surface Area = 2πrh or πdh

\n

Revolutions and Rolled Area

\n

When a cylindrical roller rolls on the ground, the flat area covered in one full revolution is exactly equal to its Curved Surface Area (CSA).

\n

Revolutions and Linear Distance

\n

If a wheel or roller moves forward, the linear distance covered in one revolution is equal to the circumference of its circular base (2πr or πd).

Radius  =1/2 m

\n\n

Length = \\(1 \\frac{1}{4}=\\frac{5}{4}\\)

\n\n

1 revolution 2πrl = 2 × \\(\\frac{22}{7} × \\frac{1}{2} × \\frac{5}{4}=\\frac{55}{14}\\)

\n\n

Maximum rolling area in 1hr = \\(\\frac{55}{14}\\) × 14 × 60 = 3300

Shortcut Trick\n

Ratio of incomes of A and B = 140 : 100 = 7 : 5

\n

Using the weighted average deviation method for net percentage increase:

\n

Net % Increase = [(7 × 25%) + (5 × 40%)] ÷ (7 + 5)

\n

Net % Increase = (175 + 200) ÷ 12 = 375 ÷ 12 = 31.25%

\n

∴ The correct answer is 31.25%.

\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

Income of A is 40% more than that of B.

\n

A gets a 25% rise in his income.

\n

B gets a 40% rise in his income.

\n

Method Used:

\n

Let B's income be a base value of 100. Calculate A's base income, determine the exact increase amount for both, and compute the combined percentage growth relative to their total base income.

\n\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ Let the initial income of B = 100

\n

⇒ Income of A = 100 + 40% of 100 = 140

\n

⇒ Total combined initial income = 140 + 100 = 240

\n

⇒ Rise in A's income = 25% of 140 = (25 ÷ 100) × 140 = 35

\n

⇒ Rise in B's income = 40% of 100 = (40 ÷ 100) × 100 = 40

\n

⇒ Total absolute increase in their combined income = 35 + 40 = 75

\n

⇒ Percentage increase = (Total Increase ÷ Combined Initial Income) × 100

\n

⇒ Percentage increase = (75 ÷ 240) × 100

\n

⇒ Percentage increase = (5 ÷ 16) × 100 = 500 ÷ 16 = 31.25%

\n

∴ The correct answer is 31.25%.

\n

Additional Information

\n

Percentage Change

\n

Percentage change represents the degree of change over time and is calculated directly using the formula: % Change = (Final Value − Initial Value) ÷ Initial Value × 100.

\n

Successive Percentage Change

\n

If a single value undergoes successive percentage changes of a% and b%, the effective overall net change is given by the formula: Net % Change = a + b + (a × b) ÷ 100.

\n

Base Value Shift in Comparison

\n

If quantity A is x% more than quantity B, then B is strictly [x ÷ (100 + x)] × 100% less than A. Always pay attention to the term following the word \"than\" or \"of\" to establish the correct base value for calculation.

Alternate Method

\n

\n

Height (h) = 7 cm

\n

Slant height (l) = 10 cm

\n

Formula Used:

\n

Radius (r) = √(l2 − h2)

\n

Volume of cone = (1/3) π r2 h

\n\n\n\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ l2 = r2 + h2

\n

⇒ 102 = r2 + 72

\n

⇒ 100 = r2 + 49

\n

⇒ r2 = 100 − 49 = 51

\n

⇒ Volume = (1/3) × (22/7) × 51 × 7

\n

⇒ Volume = (1 × 22 × 51 × 7) ÷ (3 × 7)

\n

⇒ Volume = 22 × 17

\n

⇒ Volume = 374 cm3

\n

∴ The correct answer is 374 cm3.

Additional Information

\n

Slant Height

\n

The relationship between slant height (l), radius (r), and height (h) is given by Pythagoras theorem: l2 = r2 + h2.

\n

Curved Surface Area (CSA)

\n

The lateral or curved surface area of a cone is calculated as πrl.

\n

Total Surface Area (TSA)

\n

The total surface area including the base is πrl + πr2 or πr(l + r).

\n

Volume of Cylinder Comparison

\n

The volume of a cone is exactly one-third the volume of a cylinder with the same radius and height: V = (1/3) × Volume of Cylinder. शॉर्टकट ट्रिक\n

\n

आयतन = (1/3) x (22/7) x 51 x 7 = 22 x 17 = 374

শঙ্কুর উচ্চতা = 7 সেমি

\n\n

⇒ l2= 100 = r2 + 72 

\n\n

⇒ r2 = 100 - 49 = 51

\n\n

শঙ্কুর আয়তন = \\(\\dfrac{1}{3}× \\dfrac{22}{7}× 51× 7\\)

\n\n

⇒ 22 × 17 = 374 সেমি3

\n\n

∴ উত্তরটি হল 374 সেমি3

"range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}}, "correct_option": "1", "solution_html": {"en": "\n

Shortcut Trick\n

Each of the 3 friends has 2 choices of whom to throw the ball to, making the total possible outcomes = 23 = 8.

\n

For no two friends to throw at each other, the throws must form a continuous complete cycle.

\n

There are exactly 2 possible cycles: (A → B, B → C, C → A) and (A → C, C → B, B → A).

\n

Required Probability = Favorable Outcomes ÷ Total Outcomes = 2 ÷ 8 = 1/4.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 1/4.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

Three friends throw a ball to one of the other two independently.

\n

Condition: No two friends throw balls at each other (no mutual throws).

\n\n

Concept Used:

\n

Probability = (Number of Favorable Outcomes) ÷ (Total Number of Possible Outcomes)

\n\n\n\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ Let the three friends be A, B, and C.

\n

⇒ Choices for A = 2 (can throw to B or C)

\n

⇒ Choices for B = 2 (can throw to A or C)

\n

⇒ Choices for C = 2 (can throw to A or B)

\n

⇒ Total possible outcomes = 2 × 2 × 2 = 8

\n

⇒ To ensure no two friends throw at each other, there must be NO mutual exchange (like A throws to B and B throws to A).

\n

⇒ Favorable Case 1: A throws to B, B throws to C, and C throws to A.

\n

⇒ Favorable Case 2: A throws to C, C throws to B, and B throws to A.

\n

⇒ These are the only 2 scenarios where no two persons throw at each other.

\n

⇒ Favorable outcomes = 2

\n

⇒ Required Probability = 2 ÷ 8

\n

⇒ Required Probability = 1/4

\n

∴ The correct answer is 1/4.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

Derangements

\n

A derangement is a permutation of elements where no element appears in its original position. For 3 elements, there are strictly 2 derangements, directly corresponding to the two valid cyclical throw sequences seen above.

\n

Probability of Independent Events

\n

When events are independent, the total number of combination outcomes is found by multiplying the choices for each separate event. Here, 2 options per person resulted in 2 × 2 × 2 = 8 total variations.

\n

Cyclic Permutations

\n

For n objects arranged sequentially in a circle, the number of distinct directional arrangements is (n − 1)!. For 3 objects, (3 − 1)! = 2! = 2 distinct complete cycles.

सही उत्तर 1/4 है। 

\n\n

    • \n\t
  • प्रायिकता = वांछित परिणाम/कुल परिणाम।

    • \n\t
  • मित्र A, मित्र B को फेंकता है, मित्र B, मित्र C को फेंकता है, और मित्र C, मित्र A को फेंकता है।
    • \n\t

    • \n\t
  • नतीजतन, किन्ही भी दो मित्रों द्वारा एक-दूसरे पर गेंद फेंकने की प्रायिकता 8 में से 2 या 1/4 है।

        संकल्पना :

        स्वतंत्र घटनांची संभाव्यता समजून घेणे

        • या समस्येमध्ये, तीन मित्र, प्रत्येकी बॉलसह, त्रिकोणाच्या तीन कोपऱ्यांवर उभे आहेत. प्रत्येक मित्र आपला चेंडू इतर दोन मित्रांपैकी एकाकडे स्वतंत्रपणे फेकतो.
        • कोणतेही दोन मित्र त्यांचे बॉल एकमेकांना फेकणार नाहीत याची शक्यता शोधणे हे ध्येय आहे. याचा अर्थ असा की जर मित्र A ने मित्र B कडे बॉल टाकला तर मित्र B ने बॉल परत मित्र A कडे फेकू नये आणि त्याचप्रमाणे इतर जोड्यांसाठी.

        गणना :

        • एकूण संभाव्य परिणाम: प्रत्येक मित्राकडे बॉल टाकायचा 2 पर्याय असतात (स्वतःला सोडून). अशा प्रकारे, सर्व तीन मित्रांसाठी संभाव्य परिणामांची एकूण संख्या आहे:
          • \\( 2 \\times 2 \\times 2 = 8 \\)
        • अनुकूल परिणाम: आम्ही असे निकाल शोधत आहोत जिथे कोणतेही दोन मित्र एकमेकांवर चेंडू टाकत नाहीत. चला संभाव्य मार्गांचे विश्लेषण करूया:
          • जर मित्र A ने मित्र B कडे फेकले तर मित्र B ने मित्र C कडे फेकले पाहिजे आणि मित्र C ने मित्र A वर फेकले पाहिजे. यामुळे एक चक्र तयार होते (A → B → C → A).
          • स्थिती पूर्ण करणारे केवळ दोन चक्रीय परिणाम आहेत:
            • A → B, B → C, C → A
            • A → C, C → B, B → A
          • अशा प्रकारे, 2 अनुकूल परिणाम आहेत.
        • संभाव्यता गणना: कोणत्याही दोन मित्रांनी एकमेकांवर चेंडू फेकण्याची संभाव्यता खालीलप्रमाणे दिली आहे:
          • \\( \\frac{\\text{Number of Favorable Outcomes}}{\\text{Total Outcomes}} = \\frac{2}{8} = \\frac{1}{4} \\)

          \n\t
        • এই সমস্যায় তিনজন বন্ধু প্রত্যেকে একটি বল নিয়ে একটি ত্রিভুজের তিনটি কোণে দাঁড়িয়ে আছে। প্রত্যেকে স্বাধীনভাবে অন্যদের একজনকে বল ছুঁড়ে দেয়।
          • \n\t
        • লক্ষ্য হল কোন দুই বন্ধুই একে অপরের দিকে বল ছুঁড়ে না দেওয়ার সম্ভাবনা বের করা। অর্থাৎ, যদি বন্ধু A বন্ধু B কে বল ছুঁড়ে দেয়, তাহলে বন্ধু B বন্ধু A কে বল ছুঁড়ে দেওয়া উচিত নয়, এবং অন্যান্য জোড়ার জন্যও একই।
          • \n

        \n\n
          \n\t
        • মোট সম্ভাব্য ফলাফল: প্রতিটি বন্ধুর কাছে 2 টি বিকল্প আছে যে কেউকে বল ছুঁড়ে দেওয়া যাবে (নিজেকে বাদে)। সুতরাং, তিনজন বন্ধুর জন্য মোট সম্ভাব্য ফলাফলের সংখ্যা হল:\n\n\t
            \n\t\t
          • \\( 2 \\times 2 \\times 2 = 8 \\)
            • \n\t
          \n\t
          • \n\t
        • অনুকূল ফলাফল: আমরা এমন ফলাফল খুঁজছি যেখানে কোন দুই বন্ধুই একে অপরের দিকে বল ছুঁড়ে দেয় না। আসুন সম্ভাব্য উপায়গুলি বিশ্লেষণ করি:\n\t
            \n\t\t
          • যদি বন্ধু A বন্ধু B কে বল ছুঁড়ে দেয়, বন্ধু B বন্ধু C কে বল ছুঁড়ে দিতে হবে, এবং বন্ধু C বন্ধু A কে বল ছুঁড়ে দিতে হবে। এটি একটি চক্র তৈরি করে (A → B → C → A)।

          স্বাধীন ঘটনার সম্ভাবনা বোঝা

          \n\n
            \n\t
          • এই সমস্যায় তিনজন বন্ধু প্রত্যেকে একটি বল নিয়ে একটি ত্রিভুজের তিনটি কোণে দাঁড়িয়ে আছে। প্রত্যেকে স্বাধীনভাবে অন্যদের একজনকে বল ছুঁড়ে দেয়।
            • \n\t
          • লক্ষ্য হল কোন দুই বন্ধুই একে অপরের দিকে বল ছুঁড়ে না দেওয়ার সম্ভাবনা বের করা। অর্থাৎ, যদি বন্ধু A বন্ধু B কে বল ছুঁড়ে দেয়, তাহলে বন্ধু B বন্ধু A কে বল ছুঁড়ে দেওয়া উচিত নয়, এবং অন্যান্য জোড়ার জন্যও একই।

          கருத்துரு:

          \n\n

          சுதந்திர நிகழ்வுகளின் நிகழ்தகவைப் புரிந்துகொள்வது

          \n\n
            \n\t
          • இந்தப் பிரச்சனையில், மூன்று நண்பர்கள், ஒவ்வொருவரும் ஒரு பந்தைக் கொண்டு, ஒரு முக்கோணத்தின் மூன்று மூலைகளிலும் நிற்கிறார்கள். ஒவ்வொரு நண்பரும் தங்கள் பந்தை மற்ற இரு நண்பர்களில் ஒருவருக்கு சுதந்திரமாக எறிகிறார்கள்.
            • \n\t
          • இரண்டு நண்பர்களும் ஒருவருக்கொருவர் பந்தை எறியாததற்கான நிகழ்தகவை கண்டுபிடிப்பதே இலக்கு. அதாவது, நண்பர் A நண்பர் B க்கு பந்தை எறிந்தால், நண்பர் B நண்பர் A க்கு பந்தை எறியக்கூடாது, மற்றும் மற்ற ஜோடிகளுக்கும் இதுவே பொருந்தும்.
            • \n
          \n\n

          கணக்கீடு:

          \n\n
            \n\t
          • மொத்த சாத்தியமான விளைவுகள்: ஒவ்வொரு நண்பருக்கும் (தங்களைத் தவிர்த்து) யாருக்கு பந்தை எறிய வேண்டும் என்பதற்கு 2 விருப்பங்கள் உள்ளன. எனவே, மூன்று நண்பர்களுக்கும் மொத்த சாத்தியமான விளைவுகளின் எண்ணிக்கை:\n\n\t
              \n\t\t
            • \\( 2 \\times 2 \\times 2 = 8 \\)
              • \n\t
            \n\t
            • \n\t
          • சாதகமான விளைவுகள்: எந்த இரண்டு நண்பர்களும் ஒருவருக்கொருவர் பந்தை எறியாத விளைவுகளை நாங்கள் தேடுகிறோம். சாத்தியமான வழிகளை பகுப்பாய்வு செய்வோம்:\n\t
              \n\t\t
            • நண்பர் A நண்பர் B க்கு எறிந்தால், நண்பர் B நண்பர் C க்கு எறிய வேண்டும், மற்றும் நண்பர் C நண்பர் A க்கு எறிய வேண்டும். இது ஒரு சுழற்சியை உருவாக்குகிறது (A → B → C → A).
              • \n\t\t
            • நிலையை பூர்த்தி செய்யும் இரண்டு சுழற்சி விளைவுகள் மட்டுமே உள்ளன:\n\t\t
                \n\t\t\t
              • A → B, B → C, C → A
                • \n\t\t\t
              • A → C, C → B, B → A
                • \n\t\t
              \n\t\t
              • \n\t\t
            • எனவே, 2 சாதகமான விளைவுகள் உள்ளன.

cos\\left( {12^\\circ - \\;\\theta } \\right) + \\;(ta{n^2}70^\\circ - cose{c^2}20^\\circ }}{{sin25^\\circ \\;cos65^\\circ + cos25^\\circ \\;sin65^\\circ }}\\) ਦਾ ਮੁੱਲ ਹੈ:", "options": [{"prompt": "1", "value": "-1"}, {"prompt": "2", "value": "0"}, {"prompt": "3", "value": "-2"}, {"prompt": "4", "value": "2"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}}, "correct_option": "1", "solution_html": {"en": "\n

              Shortcut Trick\n

              Put θ = 12°, sin(78° + 12°) − cos(12° − 12°) = sin 90° − cos 0° = 1 − 1 = 0.

              \n

              Also, cosec 20° = sec 70°, so tan270° − cosec220° = tan270° − sec270° = −1.

              \n

              Denominator = sin 25° cos 65° + cos 25° sin 65° = sin(25° + 65°) = sin 90° = 1.

              \n

              Value of expression = (0 − 1) ÷ 1 = −1.

              \n

              ∴ The correct answer is -1.

              \n

              Alternate Method

              \n

              Given:

              \n

              Expression: [sin(78° + θ) − cos(12° − θ) + tan270° − cosec220°] ÷ [sin 25° cos 65° + cos 25° sin 65°]

              \n

              Formula Used:

              \n

              ⇒ sin A = cos(90° − A)

              \n

              ⇒ cosec A = sec(90° − A)

              \n

              ⇒ sec2A − tan2A = 1 ⇒ tan2A − sec2A = −1

              \n

              ⇒ sin A cos B + cos A sin B = sin(A + B)

              \n

              Calculations:

              \n

              ⇒ Consider the first part of the numerator: sin(78° + θ)

              \n

              ⇒ sin(78° + θ) = cos[90° − (78° + θ)] = cos(12° − θ)

              \n

              ⇒ So, sin(78° + θ) − cos(12° − θ) = cos(12° − θ) − cos(12° − θ) = 0

              \n

              ⇒ Now, consider the second part: tan270° − cosec220°

              \n

              ⇒ cosec 20° = sec(90° − 20°) = sec 70°

              \n

              ⇒ tan270° − cosec220° = tan270° − sec270° = −1

              \n

              ⇒ Therefore, Numerator = 0 + (−1) = −1

              \n

              ⇒ Consider the denominator: sin 25° cos 65° + cos 25° sin 65°

              \n

              ⇒ Denominator = sin(25° + 65°) = sin 90° = 1

              \n

              ⇒ Value of the expression = Numerator ÷ Denominator = −1 ÷ 1 = −1

              \n

              ∴ The correct answer is -1.

              \n

              Additional Information

              \n

              Complementary Angle Identities

              \n

              When two angles add up to 90°, they are complementary. Key relations are sin A = cos(90° − A), tan A = cot(90° − A), and sec A = cosec(90° − A).

              \n

              Pythagorean Identities

              \n

              Fundamental relations between trigonometric ratios: sin2θ + cos2θ = 1, sec2θ − tan2θ = 1, and cosec2θ − cot2θ = 1.

              \n

              Compound Angle Formulas

              \n

              For any two angles A and B: sin(A + B) = sin A cos B + cos A sin B and cos(A + B) = cos A cos B − sin A sin B.

              \n\n

              ⇒ \\(\\frac{cos^220^\\circ-1}{sin^220^\\circ}\\)

              \n\n

              ⇒ - sin220°/sin220°   [∵ sin2 x + cos2 x = 1]

              \n\n

              ⇒ - 1

              \n\n

              ∴ अभीष्ट उत्तर - 1 है।

              \n\n5c:Tdbd,\n

              Shortcut Trick\n

              Let the initial number A be 100.

              \n

              First result = 100 × 1.20 × 0.70 × 1.50 = 126

              \n

              Second result = 100 × 0.80 × 1.50 × 1.10 = 132

              \n

              Required ratio = Second result ÷ First result = 132 ÷ 126 = 22/21

              \n

              ∴ The correct answer is 22/21.

              \n\n

              Alternate Method

              \n

              Given:

              \n

              Initial number = A

              \n

              Process 1: Increased by 20%, decreased by 30%, increased by 50%

              \n

              Process 2: Decreased by 20%, increased by 50%, increased by 10%

              \n\n\n\n

              Calculations:

              \n

              ⇒ First Result = A × (1 + 20/100) × (1 − 30/100) × (1 + 50/100)

              \n

              ⇒ First Result = A × 1.20 × 0.70 × 1.50 = 1.26A

              \n

              ⇒ Second Result = A × (1 − 20/100) × (1 + 50/100) × (1 + 10/100)

              \n

              ⇒ Second Result = A × 0.80 × 1.50 × 1.10 = 1.32A

              \n

              ⇒ Required Ratio = Second Result ÷ First Result

              \n

              ⇒ Required Ratio = 1.32A ÷ 1.26A

              \n

              ⇒ Required Ratio = 132 ÷ 126

              \n

              ⇒ Required Ratio = 22/21

              \n

              ∴ The correct answer is 22/21.

              \n\n

              Additional Information

              \n

              Successive Percentage Change

              \n

              When a value undergoes two successive percentage changes of x% and y%, the net equivalent percentage change is given by the formula: (x + y + xy/100)%.

              \n

              Multiplying Factor Method

              \n

              To calculate an increase of x%, multiply the base by (1 + x/100). For a decrease of y%, multiply by (1 − y/100). Successive changes can be efficiently calculated by continuously multiplying these factors together.

              \n

              Base Value Update

              \n

              In successive percentage problems, it is important to remember that each percentage change is calculated on the most recently updated base value, not the initial starting value.

              गणना:

              \n\n

              मान लीजिए कि संख्या A, "a" है,

              \n\n

              प्रश्न के अनुसार-

              \n\n

              संख्या में 20% की वृद्धि की जाती है,

              \n\n

              ⇒ 1.2 × a = 1.2a

              \n\n

              संख्या में 30% की कमी की जाती है,

              \n\n

              ⇒ 0.7 × 1.2a = 0.84a

              \n\n

              संख्या में 50% की वृद्धि की जाती है,

              \n\n

              ⇒ 1.5 × 0.84a = 1.26a

              \n\n

              इसी तरह, दूसरी स्थिति में:

              \n\n

              संख्या में 20% की कमी की जाती है,

              \n\n

              ⇒ 0.8 × a = 0.8a

              \n\n

              संख्या में 50% की वृद्धि की जाती है,

              \n\n

              ⇒ 1.5 × 0.8a = 1.2a

              \n\n

              \n\n

              \\((Result \\;2)\\over (Result \\;1)\\) = \\(1.32a\\over 1.26a\\) = \\(22\\over 21\\)

              Shortcut Trick\n

              Let the annual instalment be 100%.

              \n

              Total amount paid to clear the debt includes the simple interest earned on each instalment up to the end of 3 years.

              \n

              ⇒ Total Debt = 100% + (100% + 13.25%) + (100% + 2 × 13.25%)

              \n

              ⇒ Total Debt = 100% + 113.25% + 126.5% = 339.75%

              \n

              ⇒ 339.75% = Rs. 8154 ⇒ 100% = (8154 ÷ 339.75) × 100 = Rs. 2400

              \n

              ∴ The correct answer is Rs. 2,400.

              \n\n

              Alternate Method

              \n

              Given:

              \n

              Debt (A) = Rs. 8,154

              \n

              Time (T) = 3 years

              \n

              Rate (R) = 13.25% p.a.

              \n

              Formula Used:

              \n

              Annual Instalment = (100 × A) ÷ [100 × T + (R × T × (T − 1)) ÷ 2]

              \n\n\n\n

              Calculations:

              \n

              ⇒ Annual Instalment = (100 × 8154) ÷ [100 × 3 + (13.25 × 3 × 2) ÷ 2]

              \n

              ⇒ Annual Instalment = 815400 ÷ [300 + 39.75]

              \n

              ⇒ Annual Instalment = 815400 ÷ 339.75

              \n

              ⇒ Annual Instalment = 2400

              \n

              ∴ The correct answer is Rs. 2,400.

              \n\n

              Additional Information

              \n

              Simple Interest Instalment

              \n

              Instalments paid earlier earn interest until the end of the term. For a debt due in n years, the first instalment earns interest for (n − 1) years, while the last instalment earns none.

              \n

              Compound Interest Instalment

              \n

              When clearing a loan with compound interest, the present value of all instalments equals the principal borrowed: P = x ÷ (1 + R ÷ 100) + x ÷ (1 + R ÷ 100)2 + ...

              \n

              Present Worth and True Discount

              \n

              Present Worth (PW) is the actual borrowed amount. The Debt (A) is the sum of Present Worth and True Discount (TD), where TD = (PW × R × T) ÷ 100.

              \n\n

              Shortcut Trick\n

              From the first condition: 90° − x = 180° − 4x → 3x = 90° → x = 30°

              \n

              From the second condition: 180° − y = 4 × (90° − y) → 3y = 180° → y = 60°

              \n

              Required sum = x + y = 30° + 60° = 90°

              \n

              ∴ The correct answer is 90°.

              \n

              Alternate Method

              \n

              Given:

              \n

              Complement of an angle x = Supplement of angle 4x

              \n

              Supplem", "hn": "

              प्रयुक्त अवधारणा : 

              \n\n

              θ1 + θ2 = 90° [पूरक कोण]

              \n\n

              θ1 + θ2 = 180° [संपूरक कोण]

              \n\n

              गणना : 

              \n\n

              प्रश्नानुसार,

              \n\n

              ⇒ 90° - x = 180° - 4x --------(1)

              \n\n

              ⇒ 180° - y = 4(90° - y)   ------------(2)

              \n\n

"correct_option": "4", "solution_html": {"en": "\n

              Shortcut Trick\n

              Multiplying factor for 15% compound interest annually = 1 + 15/100 = 115/100 = 23/20.

              \n

              Ratio of Amount to Principal = 279841 ÷ 160000.

              \n

              We know the denominator 160000 is exactly 204, so we check if 279841 is 234.

              \n

              Since 232 = 529 and 529 × 529 = 279841, the ratio is strictly (23/20)4.

              \n

              The exponent represents the number of compounding years.

              \n

              ∴ The correct answer is 4 years.

              \n\n

              Alternate Method

              \n

              Given:

              \n

              Principal (P) = ₹1,60,000

              \n

              Amount (A) = ₹2,79,841

              \n

              Rate of Interest (R) = 15% per annum compounded annually

              \n

              Time = n years

              \n

              Formula Used:

              \n

              A = P × (1 + R/100)n

              \n\n\n\n

              Calculations:

              \n

              ⇒ 279841 = 160000 × (1 + 15/100)n

              \n

              ⇒ 279841 ÷ 160000 = (115/100)n

              \n

              ⇒ 279841 ÷ 160000 = (23/20)n

              \n

              ⇒ Observing the denominator: 160000 = 16 × 10000 = 24 × 104 = 204

              \n

              ⇒ Similarly, analyzing the numerator: 232 = 529 and 529 × 529 = 279841, therefore 279841 = 234

              \n

              ⇒ (23/20)4 = (23/20)n

              \n

              ⇒ By comparing the bases and equating the powers, we get n = 4.

              \n

              ∴ The correct answer is 4 years.

              \n\n

              Additional Information

              \n

              Half-Yearly Compounding

              \n

              If the interest is compounded half-yearly instead of annually, the applied rate is halved (R/2)% and the time period becomes doubled (2n) in the CI formula.

              \n

              Effective Rate of Compound Interest

              \n

              For 2 years at an annual rate of R%, the effective compound interest percentage can be directly calculated using the successive percentage formula: (2R + R2/100)%.

              \n

              Difference Between CI and SI

              \n

              For exactly 2 years, the difference between CI and SI on the same principal is given by: CI − SI = P × (R/100)2. For 3 years, the difference formula extends to: P × (R/100)2 × (3 + R/100).

              दिया है:

              \n\n

              मूलधन राशि (P) = 1,60,000 रुपये

              \n\n

              मिश्रधन (A) = 2,79,841 रुपये

              \n\n

              ब्याज दर (R) = 15% प्रति वर्ष

              \n\n

              प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

              \n\n

              मिश्रधन = मूलधन × (1 + दर/100)समय

              \n\n

              गणना:

              \n\n

              2,79,841 = 1,60,000 × (1 + 15/100)n

              \n\n

              ⇒ (1 + 15/100)n = 2,79,841 / 1,60,000

              \n\n

              ⇒ (115/100)n = 2,79,841 / 1,60,000

              \n\n

              ⇒ (115/100)n = 1.74900625

              \n\n

              ⇒ (1.15)n = 1.74900625

              \n\n

              ⇒ n log(1.15) = log(1.74900625)

              \n\n

              ⇒ n = log(1.74900625) / log(1.15)

              \n\n

              ⇒ n ≈ 4

              \n\n

              समय अवधि लगभग 4 वर्ष है।

              \n\n

              মূলধনের পরিমাণ (P) = 1,60,000 টাকা।

The sum on which the simple interest is calculated is: ", "options": [{"prompt": "1", "value": "Rs. 1,640"}, {"prompt": "2", "value": "Rs. 2,450"}, {"prompt": "3", "value": "Rs. 1,680"}, {"prompt": "4", "value": "Rs. 2,000"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}, "hn": {"value": "एक निश्चित मूलधन पर \\(4\\frac{1}{2}\\) वर्षों के लिए 10% प्रति वर्ष की दर से साधारण ब्याज, 12,000 रुपये के मूलधन पर 10% प्रति वर्ष की दर से 1 वर्ष के चक्रवृद्धि ब्याज का 60% है, ब्याज अर्धवार्षिक रूप से संयोजित होता है। वह मूलधन कितना है, जिस पर साधारण ब्याज की गणना की जाती है?: ", "options": [{"prompt": "1", "value": "1,640&nbsp;<span style="">रुपये</span>"}, {"prompt": "2", "value": "2,450&nbsp;<span style="">रुपये</span>"}, {"prompt": "3", "value": "1,680&nbsp;<span style="">रुपये</span>"}, {"prompt": "4", "value": "2,000&nbsp;<span style="">रुपये</span>"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}}, "correct_option": "1", "solution_html": {"en": "\n

              Shortcut Trick\n

              Effective rate for CI at 10% p.a. compounded half-yearly for 1 year = 5% + 5% + (5×5)/100 = 10.25%

              \n

              Total Compound Interest = 10.25% of 12000 = Rs. 1230

              \n

              Given Simple Interest = 60% of CI = 0.60 × 1230 = Rs. 738

              \n

              Using SI = (P × R × T) / 100 ⇒ P × 10 × 4.5 / 100 = 738

              \n

              P × 0.45 = 738 ⇒ P = 1640

              \n

              ∴ The correct answer is Rs. 1640.

              \n\n

              Alternate Method

              \n

              Given:

              \n

              CI Principal = Rs. 12000

              \n

              CI Rate = 10% p.a. (5% per half-year), Time = 1 year (2 half-years)

              \n

              SI Rate = 10% p.a., Time = 4.5 years (9/2 ye", "hn": "\n

              सूत्र:

              \n\n

              SI = \\(P×R× T\\over 100\\)

              \n\n

              A = P × (1 + \\(R\\over 100\\))T

              \n\n

              CI = A - P

              \n\n

              जहाँ-

              \n\n

              P = मूलधन

              \n\n

              SI = साधारण ब्याज

              \n\n

              R = दर

              \n\n

              T = समय

              \n\n

              CI = चक्रवृद्धि ब्याज

              \n\n

              A = मिश्रधन

              \n\n

              गणना:

              \n\n

              माना कि मूलधन P है

              \n\n

              SI = \\(P × 10 × {9\\over2} \\over100\\) = \\(9P\\over20\\)

              \n\n

              चक्रवृद्धि ब्याज से मिश्रधन = 12,000 × (1 + \\(5\\over 100\\))2

              \n\n

              चक्रवृद्धि ब्याज से मिश्रधन = 12,000 × \\(21\\over20\\) × \\(21\\over20\\) =  13,230 रुपये

              \n\n

              CI =  13,230 रुपये - 12,000 रुपये = 1230 रुपये

              \n\n

              प्रश्नानुसार-

              \n\n

              ⇒ \\(9P\\over20\\) =  \\(60\\over100\\) × 1230 रुपये

              \n\n

              ⇒ P = 1640 रुपये

              \n\n

              ∴ अभीष्ट मूलधन 1640 रुपये है।

              Shortcut Trick\n

              Shortcut Trick\n

              Put A = 45°. Then tan(45°) = 1 and sin(45°) = 1/√2.

              \n

              p = 1 + 1/√2 and q = 1 − 1/√2

              \n

              p2 − q2 = (p + q) × (p − q) = (2) × (2/√2) = 2√2

              \n

              pq = (1 + 1/√2) × (1 − 1/√2) = 1 − 1/2 = 1/2

              \n

              4√(pq) = 4 × √(1/2) = 4/√2 = 2√2

              \n

              ∴ The correct answer is p2 − q2 = 4√(pq).

              \n\n

              Alternate Method

              \n

              Given:

              \n

              p = tanA + sinA

              \n

              q = tanA − sinA

              \n

              Calculations:

              \n

              ⇒ p + q = 2tanA

              \n

              ⇒ p − q = 2sinA

              \n

              ⇒ p2 − q2 = (p + q) × (p − q) = (2tanA) × (2sinA) = 4tanA × sinA

              \n

              Now, let us calculate the product pq:

              \n

              ⇒ pq = (tanA + sinA) × (tanA − sinA)

              \n

              ⇒ pq = tan2A − sin2A

              \n

              ⇒ pq = (sin2A ÷ cos2A) − sin2A

              \n

              ⇒ pq = sin2A × (1/cos2A − 1)

              \n

              ⇒ pq = sin2A × (sec2A − 1)

              \n

              ⇒ pq = sin2A × tan2A

              \n

              ⇒ √(pq) = sinA × tanA

              \n

              Multiplying by 4 on both sides:

              \n

              ⇒ 4√(pq) = 4tanA × sinA

              \n

              Comparing the values of (p2 − q2) and 4√(pq):

              \n

              ⇒ p2 − q2 = 4√(pq)

              \n

              ∴ The correct answer is p2 − q2 = 4√(pq).

              \n\n

              Additional Information

              \n

              Trigonometric Identities

              \n

              The three fundamental identities are: sin2θ + cos2θ = 1, sec2θ − tan2θ = 1, and cosec2θ − cot2θ = 1.

              \n

              Difference of Squares

              \n

              The algebraic identity a2 − b2 = (a − b) × (a + b) helps simplify quadratic differences rapidly.

              \n

              Tangent Formula

              \n

              The relationship tanθ = sinθ ÷ cosθ is useful for converting all terms into sine and cosine to simplify complex equations.

              दिया गया​ है:

              \n\n

              tanA + sinA = p और tanA - sinA = q

              \n\n

              गणना:

              \n\n

              tanA + sinA = p और tanA - sinA = q

              \n\n

              हमारे पास है,

              \n\n

              p = (tanA + sinA)

              \n\n

              और, q = tanA – sinA

              \n\n

              अब,

              \n\n

              ⇒ p2 – q2 = (tanA + sinA)2 – (tanA – sinA)2

              \n\n

              ⇒ (tanA + sinA + tanA – sinA) (tanA + sinA – tanA + sinA)

              \n\n

              ⇒ 2 tanA × 2sinA

              \n\n

              ⇒ 4 tanA sinA 

              \n\n

              ⇒ 4 √(tan2A sin2A)

              \n\n

              ⇒ 4 √(sec2A – 1)sinA       

              \n\n

              [∴ sec2θ – tan2θ = 1]

              \n\n

              ⇒ 4 √(sec2A.sin2A – sin2A)

              \n\n

              ⇒ 4 √(sin2A/cos2A) – sin2A

              \n\n

              ⇒ 4 √(tan2A – sin2A)

              \n\n

              ⇒ 4 √(tanA + sinA) (tanA – sinA)

              \n\n

              ⇒ 4 √pq

              \n\n

              ∴ अभीष्ट मान p2 - q2 = 4√pq है।

20m ਅਤੇ 15m ਦੇ ਆਇਤਾਕਾਰ ਪਾਰਕ ਦੇ ਅੰਦਰ 1m ਦੀ ਇਕਸਾਰ ਚੌੜਾਈ ਦਾ ਇੱਕ ਰਸਤਾ ਬਣਾਇਆ ਗਿਆ ਹੈ। ਰਸਤੇ ਦਾ ਖੇਤਰਫਲ ਪਤਾ ਕਰੋ।

Shortcut Trick\n

Area of a path constructed inside a rectangle is given by: Area = 2w(L + B − 2w)

\n

Where L = 20 m, B = 15 m, w = 1 m

\n

⇒ Area = 2 × 1 × (20 + 15 − 2 × 1)

\n

⇒ Area = 2 × (35 − 2)

\n

⇒ Area = 2 × 33 = 66 m2

\n

∴ The correct answer is 66 m2.

\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

Length of the outer park (L) = 20 m

\n

Breadth of the outer park (B) = 15 m

\n

Width of the inner path (w) = 1 m

\n\n

Formula Used:

\n

Area of rectangle = Length × Breadth

\n

Area of inner path = Area of outer rectangle − Area of inner rectangle

\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ Area of outer rectangular park = 20 × 15 = 300 m2

\n

Since the path of 1 m width is made inside, the dimensions of the inner rectangle will decrease by 2 m (1 m from each side).

\n

⇒ Inner length (l) = 20 − (2 × 1) = 18 m

\n

⇒ Inner breadth (b) = 15 − (2 × 1) = 13 m

\n

⇒ Area of inner rectangle = 18 × 13 = 234 m2

\n

⇒ Area of path = 300 − 234 = 66 m2

\n

∴ The correct answer is 66 m2.

\n

Additional Information

\n

Path Inside a Rectangle

\n

When a path of width w is constructed inside a rectangular park of length L and breadth B, Area of path = 2w(L + B − 2w).

\n

Path Outside a Rectangle

\n

When a path of width w is constructed outside a rectangular park of length L and breadth B, Area of path = 2w(L + B + 2w).

\n

Cross Path Inside a Rectangle

\n

When two mutually perpendicular paths of width w cross each other centrally inside a rectangle, Area of path = w(L + B − w).

दिया गया है:

\n\n

20 मीटर और 15 मीटर के आयताकार पार्क के अंदर 1 मीटर की समान चौड़ाई का एक पथ बनाया गया है।

\n\n

प्रयुक्त अवधारणा:

\n\n

आयताकार पार्क का क्षेत्रफल = लंबाई × चौड़ाई

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

\n\n

पार्क 20 मीटर x 15 मीटर की माप वाला एक आयत है। 

\n\n

अब, पूरे पार्क का क्षेत्रफल = 20 × 15 = 300 वर्ग मीटर

\n\n

⇒ आंतरिक आयत की लंबाई = 20 - 2 = 18 मीटर

\n\n

⇒ आंतरिक आयत की चौड़ाई = 15 - 2 = 13 मीटर

\n\n

आंतरिक आयत का क्षेत्रफल

\n\n

⇒ 18 मीटर × 13 मीटर = 234 वर्ग मीटर

\n\n

पथ का क्षेत्रफल = (पार्क का कुल क्षेत्रफल) - (आंतरिक आयत का क्षेत्रफल)

\n\n

⇒ 300 वर्ग मीटर (पार्क का कुल क्षेत्रफल) - 234 वर्ग मीटर (आंतरिक आयत का क्षेत्रफल)

\n\n

⇒ 66 वर्ग मीटर

\n\n

∴ पथ का अभीष्ट क्षेत्रफल 66 वर्ग मीटर है।

दिलेले आहे​:

\n\n

20 मीटर आणि 15 मीटर च्या आयताकृती उद्यानाच्या आत 1 मीटर रुंदीचा मार्ग तयार केला आहे.

\n\n

वापरलेले सूत्र:

\n\n

आयताकृती उद्यानाचे क्षेत्रफळ = लांबी × रुंदी

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

\n\n

उद्यान 20 मीटर x 15 मीटर आकाराचे आयत आहे

\n\n

आता, संपूर्ण उद्यानाचे क्षेत्रफळ = 20 × 15 = 300 चौरस मीटर.

\n\n

⇒ आतील आयताची लांबी = 20 - 2 = 18 मीटर

\n\n

⇒ आतील आयताची रुंदी = 15 - 2 = 13 मीटर

\n\n

आतील आयताचे क्षेत्रफळ

\n\n

⇒ 18 मीटर × 13 मीटर = 234 चौरस मीटर.

\n\n

मार्गाचे क्षेत्रफळ = (उद्यानाचे एकूण क्षेत्रफळ) - (आतील आयताचे क्षेत्रफळ)

\n\n

⇒ 300 चौरस मीटर (एकूण उद्यान क्षेत्र) - 234 चौरस मीटर (आतील आयताचे क्षेत्र)

\n\n

⇒ 66 चौरस मीटर

\n\n

∴ मार्गाचे क्षेत्रफळ 66 चौरस मीटर आहे.

આપેલ:

\n\n

20 મીટર અને 15 મીટરના લંબચોરસ ઉદ્યાનની અંદર 1 મીટરની સમાન પહોળાઈનો રસ્તો બનાવવામાં આવ્યો છે.

\n\n

વપરાયેલ સૂત્ર:

\n\n

લંબચોરસ ઉદ્યાનનું ક્ષેત્રફળ = લંબાઈ × પહોળાઈ

\n\n

ગણતરી:

\n\n

\n\n

આ ઉદ્યાન 20 મીટર x 15 મીટરનું લંબચોરસ છે

\n\n

હવે, સમગ્ર ઉદ્યાનનું ક્ષેત્રફળ = 20 × 15 = 300 ચોરસ મીટર.

\n\n

⇒ અંદરના લંબચોરસની લંબાઈ = 20 - 2 = 18 m

\n\n

⇒ આંતરિક લંબચોરસની પહોળાઈ = 15 - 2 = 13 m

\n\n

અંદરના લંબચોરસનું ક્ષેત્રફળ

\n\n

⇒ 18 મી × 13 મી = 234 ચોરસ મીટર.

கொடுக்கப்பட்டது:

\n\n

பயன்படுத்திய சூத்திரம்:

\n\n

செவ்வக பூங்காவின் பரப்பளவு = நீளம் × அகலம்

\n\n

கணக்கீடு:

\n\n

\n\n

பூங்கா 20 மீ x 15 மீ அளவுள்ள ஒரு செவ்வகமாகும்

\n\n

இப்போது, முழு பூங்காவின் பரப்பளவு = 20 × 15 = 300 சதுர மீட்டர்.

\n\n

⇒ உள் செவ்வகத்தின் நீளம் = 20 - 2 = 18 மீ

\n\n

⇒ உள் செவ்வகத்தின் அகலம் = 15 - 2 = 13 மீ

\n\n

உள் செவ்வகத்தின் பரப்பளவு.

\n\n

⇒ 18மீ × 13மீ = 234 சதுர மீட்டர்.

\n\n

பாதையின் பரப்பளவு = (பூங்காவின் மொத்த பரப்பளவு) - (உள் செவ்வகத்தின் பரப்பளவு).

\n\n

⇒ 300 சதுர மீட்டர் (மொத்த பூங்கா பரப்பளவு) - 234 சதுர மீட்டர் (உள் செவ்வக பரப்பளவு).

\n\n

⇒ 66 சதுர மீட்டர்.

\n\n

∴ பாதையின் பரப்பளவு 66 சதுர மீட்டர்.

\na7:T9ef,\n

ଦିଆଯାଇଛି:

\n\n

20 ମିଟର ଏବଂ 15 ମିଟରର ଆୟତାକାର ପାର୍କ ଭିତରେ 1 ମିଟର ପ୍ରସ୍ଥର ଏକ ରାସ୍ତା ତିଆରି କରାଯାଇଛି । 

\n\n

ବ୍ୟବହୃତ ସୂତ୍ର:

\n\n

ଆୟତାକାର ପାର୍କର କ୍ଷେତ୍ରଫଳ  = ଦୈର୍ଘ୍ୟ x ପ୍ରସ୍ଥ |

\n\n

ଗଣନା:

\n\n

\n\n

ପାର୍କଟି ହେଉଛି ଏକ ଆୟତକ୍ଷେତ୍ର ଯାହାକି 20 ମିଟର x 15 ମିଟର ଅଟେ । 

\n\n

ବର୍ତ୍ତମାନ, ପୁରା ପାର୍କର କ୍ଷେତ୍ରଫଳ  = 20 x 15 = 300 ବର୍ଗ ମିଟର |

\n\n

⇒ ଆଭ୍ୟନ୍ତରୀଣ ଆୟତକ୍ଷେତ୍ରର ଲମ୍ବ = 20 - 2 = 18 ମି

\n\n

⇒ ଆଭ୍ୟନ୍ତରୀଣ ଆୟତକ୍ଷେତ୍ରର ମୋଟେଇ = 15 - 2 = 13 ମିଦିଆଯାଇଛି:

\n\n

20 ମିଟର ଏବଂ 15 ମିଟରର ଆୟତାକାର ପାର୍କ ଭିତରେ 1 ମିଟର ପ୍ରସ୍ଥର ଏକ ରାସ୍ତା ତିଆରି କରାଯାଇଛି । 

\n\n

ବ୍ୟବହୃତ ସୂତ୍ର:

\n\n

ଆୟତାକାର ପାର୍କର କ୍ଷେତ୍ରଫଳ  = ଦୈର୍ଘ୍ୟ x ପ୍ରସ୍ଥ |

\n\n

ଗଣନା:

\n\n

\n\n

ପାର୍କଟି ହେଉଛି ଏକ ଆୟତକ୍ଷେତ୍ର ଯାହାକି 20 ମିଟର x 15 ମିଟର ଅଟେ । 

\n\n

ବର୍ତ୍ତମାନ, ପୁରା ପାର୍କର କ୍ଷେତ୍ରଫଳ  = 20 x 15 = 300 ବର୍ଗ ମିଟର |

\n\n

⇒ ଆଭ୍ୟନ୍ତରୀଣ ଆୟତକ୍ଷେତ୍ରର ଲମ୍ବ = 20 - 2 = 18 ମି

\n\n

⇒ ଆଭ୍ୟନ୍ତରୀଣ ଆୟତକ୍ଷେତ୍ରର ମୋଟେଇ = 15 - 2 = 13 ମି

\n\n

ଭିତର ଆୟତକ୍ଷେତ୍ରର କ୍ଷେତ୍ରଫଳ

\n\n

⇒ 18 ମି x 13 ମି = 234 ବର୍ଗ ମିଟର 

\n\n

ପଥର କ୍ଷେତ୍ର = (ପାର୍କର ସମୁଦାୟ କ୍ଷେତ୍ରଫଳ) - (ଭିତର ଆୟତକ୍ଷେତ୍ରର କ୍ଷେତ୍ରଫଳ)

\n\n

⇒ 300 ବର୍ଗ ମିଟର (ସମୁଦାୟ ପାର୍କର କ୍ଷେତ୍ରଫଳ) - 234 ବର୍ଗ ମିଟର (ଭିତର ଆୟତକ୍ଷେତ୍ରର କ୍ଷେତ୍ରଫଳ)

\n\n

⇒ 66 ବର୍ଗ ମିଟର 

\n\n

∴ ଏହି ରାସ୍ତାର କ୍ଷେତ୍ରଫଳ ହେଉଛି 66 ବର୍ଗ ମିଟର ।

\n\n

20m ਅਤੇ 15m ਦੇ ਆਇਤਾਕਾਰ ਪਾਰਕ ਦੇ ਅੰਦਰ 1m ਦੀ ਇਕਸਾਰ ਚੌੜਾਈ ਦਾ ਇੱਕ ਰਸਤਾ ਬਣਾਇਆ ਗਿਆ ਹੈ।

\n\n

\n\n

ਗਣਨਾ:

\n\n

\n\n

ਪਾਰਕ 20 m x 15 m ਮਾਪ ਦਾ ਇੱਕ ਆਇਤ ਹੈ।

\n\n

ਹੁਣ, ਪੂਰੇ ਪਾਰਕ ਦਾ ਖੇਤਰਫਲ = 20 x 15 = 300 ਵਰਗ ਮੀਟਰ।

\n\n

Shortcut Trick\n

Observe the antecedent (first term) and consequent (second term) of each given ratio.

\n

Only in the ratio 6 : 5 is the antecedent strictly greater than the consequent.

\n

This means 6 : 5 evaluates to a value greater than 1, while all other given ratios (3 : 5, 4 : 5, 7 : 10) are proper fractions less than 1.

\n

Thus, without any complex calculation, we can determine the largest value.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 6 : 5.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

Four ratios: 3 : 5, 6 : 5, 4 : 5, and 7 : 10

\n\n\n\n

Method Used:

\n

Convert each ratio into its equivalent decimal value by dividing the first term by the second term.

\n\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ 3 : 5 = 3 ÷ 5 = 0.6

\n

⇒ 6 : 5 = 6 ÷ 5 = 1.2

\n

⇒ 4 : 5 = 4 ÷ 5 = 0.8

\n

⇒ 7 : 10 = 7 ÷ 10 = 0.7

\n

⇒ Comparing the calculated decimal values: 1.2 > 0.8 > 0.7 > 0.6

\n

⇒ The largest decimal value is 1.2, which directly corresponds to the ratio 6 : 5.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 6 : 5.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

Comparing by Denominator Equalization (LCM Method)

\n

Fractions can be rapidly compared by equating their denominators. The LCM of 5 and 10 is 10. Expanding the given ratios yields 6/10, 12/10, 8/10, and 7/10. Comparing numerators: 12 > 8 > 7 > 6, confirming 6 : 5 is the maximum.

\n\n

Proper vs Improper Ratios

\n

A ratio a : b where a < b behaves like a proper fraction (less than 1). When a > b, it forms an improper fraction (greater than 1). Spotting this visually saves valuable examination time.

\n\n

Percentage Conversion Technique

\n

Converting ratios to percentages using Base × 100 provides a simple uniform scale. Applying this gives 60%, 120%, 80%, and 70%. Comparing whole integers helps to eliminate decimal point calculation errors.
